=== INSTRUCTIONS FOR CLAUDE (read first) ===
You are auditing "Career Mode Showdown", a private two-player FIFA 17 career tracker (Daniel and Nik, one phone or laptop each). It is a static site on GitHub Pages with Firebase Auth and Firestore on the free Spark plan (50,000 reads and 20,000 writes per day, no Cloud Functions). Goal: 10 smooth seasons per Showdown and 20 to 30 seasons a day inside the free limit. House rules any fix must keep: equal scores are a DRAW (no tiebreak anywhere); Showdowns are 1, 3, 5 or 10 seasons; Europa League +3 and Champions League +5, never both; nothing may dim or cover the screen; the loading screen stays until the next screen is ready. Out of scope: attacks that need a modified app or the browser console, style, naming and refactors.

AREA: The r85 release (v1.9.9). The first file is its release note and the second is the full code diff from r84 to r85. Check what the note claims against the diff, and review every changed line for regressions: music player, crests, season acknowledgement race, the SSJR test panel, the reads counter, and the stricter Rules for shared writes.

Answer with at most 12 findings, most severe first. For each: severity P0 to P3, the file and function, what goes wrong for the players, how normal play triggers it, and the smallest fix. Then list what you checked and found sound. Keep the answer under 1,500 words. Only report what the code below actually shows; say "unsure" when a file you would need is not included.
=== END INSTRUCTIONS ===

=== FILE: RELEASE_V1.9.9_R85.md ===
# Career Mode Showdown — r85: marble music player, premium club crests, safer shared writes (v1.9.9 runtime r85)

Application version: `v1.9.9`  
Runtime asset revision: `1.9.9-r85`  
Previous known-good runtime: `1.9.8-r84`

r85 builds on r84. The application version moves to 1.9.9 and the runtime revision to r85.

## What changed

- Music: nine new songs, with Blur "Song 2" first and John Newman "Love Me Again" second. A marble player sits in the
  middle of the desktop top bar on every screen that has the top bar; Settings has none. On phones it is the MUSIC tab
  in the bottom bar plus Home TRACKS. The tracklist is a readable, keyboard-friendly list with the current song marked
  (#1010, #1018).
- Premium club crests: 98 drawn crests, with no lettering, on Club, Standings, Rivalry and Legacy (#1012).
- Settings SEASONS LEFT TODAY starts from the trimmed season cost (#1011).
- Season acknowledgement race: a second proving read after 400 ms, so a tap made just before the rival's acknowledgement
  is recorded (#1009).
- SSJR test panel (`?ssjr=1`) records the real two-phone run itself, restarts after a closed, failed run, and has
  readable buttons (#1014, #1020).
- Reads counter: direct gets and listeners are counted (#1016).
- Docs: the R84 correction note, current entry points, and scoring flags that say draws (#1015).

## Update safety

- The shell and service worker advance to r85 in a fresh `career-mode-showdown-shell-1.9.9-r85` cache, and r84 is retained
  for rollback. The new cache revision also stops old crests from flashing after the update.

## What did not change

- The scoring formula and the Remote Joining protocol stay as they were. No stored document shape changes. No tiebreak.
- The no-flash rules, the Reus Gate, preload and decode are unchanged.
- Firebase remains Spark-only, billing remains OFF and App Check enforcement remains OFF.
- No saved Showdown is rewritten. Nothing in Firebase is deleted or edited.

## Firestore Rules

- Rules change (audit R84 F01/F02, PR #1017). Every shared write (setup, transfers, results, season commit create and
  acknowledge) now checks the acting account is active, the acting device is registered and active, and the session
  belongs to both members (`ssjrActorWriteAuthorityValid`). The unused `ssjrCommitShape` helper is deleted. Reads stay
  membership-only. Cost: about 28 more billed Rules lookups per season.
- Generated Rules at the release head: `firestore.spark.generated.rules`, 123,027 bytes, SHA-256
  `b6a3206e0b1f4296a12450e7670feba20e5902e727aa8e996b7ef9fa6cf20f19`.
- Rollback pair: rolling back only the app does not roll back the Rules. The r84 rollback pair is the r84 app with the
  r84 generated Rules (SHA-256 `36d58d21147004f3c768cdadb219c4617f78d0c312b74207b6606844cc98b131`).

## Checks

- Filled in on the release PR from the exact release head.

## SSJR status

SSJR-1.1 remains frozen at `0/100`. SSJR-2.1 is at `0/100` until a validated two-device production run is recorded. No
SSJR credit comes from this source, CI or deployment. No physical acceptance is claimed for r85.

## Correction (2026-10-27, Rules hash)

The "Firestore Rules" section above names `firestore.spark.generated.rules` (SHA-256 `b6a3206e…`). That file is a
build input, not what is deployed. The Deploy Firebase Firestore Rules workflow composes the deployed artifact, and the
Showdown Gate L5 check (T9/M12) pins that composed file:

- r85 deployed Rules, composed at main `67479f28`: 153,241 bytes, SHA-256
  `8ea8d7ba9bf89ad999969da961b5b748e62bdb3348b7fe609cf3da2f67b71f61`.
- r84 deployed Rules, composed at main `5b0ab3ee` / `cd7df26e`: 156,903 bytes, SHA-256
  `686c87f432be7371a595ec7822ffd7f2bbc12f5ad809296abaed636785ed94b4`. This is the value the independent R84 audit
  recorded, so the audit was right; the `36d58d21…` value in the R84 correction is the generated build input.

The coherent rollback pair is the r84 app with the r84 composed Rules above.

+# Claude review (Nik 2026-10-27): an outside Claude code review, paid from Nik's own Claude Console credit (repo secret
+# ANTHROPIC_API_KEY), next to the Codex reviews. It spends only where a miss could hurt a 10-season career:
+#   Opus 5.5:   release PRs into main, and anything touching Firestore Rules, sign-in, pairing, sync or the service worker.
+#   Sonnet 5.5: other game-logic JavaScript (scoring, season saves, career history).
+#   Skipped:    looks only (CSS, pictures, *V10* screen files, music), tests, docs, job files and drafts.
+# It runs once when a PR opens or leaves draft, not on every push; "@claude review" in a PR comment asks again.
+# It never changes code: it reads the PR and posts its findings as comments. It is not a required check.
+name: Claude Review
+
+on:
+  pull_request:
+    types: [opened, ready_for_review]
+  issue_comment:
+    types: [created]
+
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+  pull-requests: read
+
+jobs:
+  review:
+    name: Claude review
+    if: >-
+      ${{ (github.event_name == 'pull_request' && github.event.pull_request.draft == false) ||
+          (github.event_name == 'issue_comment' && github.event.issue.pull_request &&
+           startsWith(github.event.comment.body, '@claude review') &&
+           contains(fromJSON('["OWNER","MEMBER","COLLABORATOR"]'), github.event.comment.author_association)) }}
+    # On the job, after its filter: an ordinary PR comment never cancels a review that is already running (Codex #1013).
+    concurrency:
+      group: claude-review-${{ github.event.pull_request.number || github.event.issue.number }}
+      cancel-in-progress: true
+    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
+    timeout-minutes: 30
+    permissions:
+      contents: read
+      pull-requests: write
+      issues: write
+      id-token: write
+    steps:
+      - name: Choose the reviewer
+        id: plan
+        shell: bash
+        env:
+          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
+          REPO: ${{ github.repository }}
+          PR: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number || github.event.issue.number }}
+          EVENT: ${{ github.event_name }}
+        run: |
+          set -euo pipefail
+          base=$(gh api "repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR" --jq .base.ref)
+          head=$(gh api "repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR" --jq .head.sha)
+          files=$(gh api --paginate "repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR/files?per_page=100" --jq '.[].filename')
+          code=$(printf '%s\n' "$files" | grep -E '(^js/.*\.js$|\.rules$|^firebase\.json$|^firestore.*\.json$|^service-worker\.js$)' || true)
+          risky=$(printf '%s\n' "$code" | grep -Ei '(\.rules$|\.json$|service-worker|pairing|auth|sync|signin|session|credential|firestore|firebase|budget)' || true)
+          logic=$(printf '%s\n' "$code" | grep -E '^js/' | grep -Ev '(V10|musicDock)' || true)
+          model=""
+          if [ "$base" = "main" ] || [ -n "$risky" ]; then model="claude-opus-5-5"
+          elif [ -n "$logic" ]; then model="claude-sonnet-5-5"
+          elif [ "$EVENT" = "issue_comment" ]; then model="claude-sonnet-5-5"
+          fi
+          echo "base=$base head=$head model=${model:-skip}"
+          printf '%s\n' "$files" | head -50
+          {
+            echo "model=$model"
+            echo "head=$head"
+            echo "pr=$PR"
+          } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
+
+      - name: Nothing to review
+        if: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.model == '' }}
+        run: echo "Only looks, tests or docs changed; Claude review skipped."
+
+      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5
+        if: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.model != '' }}
+        with:
+          ref: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.head }}
+          fetch-depth: 1
+          persist-credentials: false
+
+      - name: Claude review
+        if: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.model != '' }}
+        uses: anthropics/claude-code-action@8041f1d17e0e7d2f2cfe35002962e9f26237c0bb # v1
+        with:
+          anthropic_api_key: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
+          track_progress: false
+          use_sticky_comment: true
+          prompt: |
+            REPO: ${{ github.repository }}
+            PR NUMBER: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.pr }}
+            The PR head is checked out in the working directory. Review only what this PR changes (gh pr diff).
+
+            The game: Career Mode Showdown, a private two-player FIFA 17 career tracker (Daniel and Nik, one phone or
+            laptop each), static site on GitHub Pages with Firebase on the free Spark plan. The goal is 10 smooth seasons.
+
+            Report only real defects, most severe first, at most 8: wrong results, broken game flow, lost or corrupted
+            career data, sync races between the two devices, Firestore Rules holes that let one player break the other's
+            game, extra Firestore reads or writes per season (the free daily limit is tight), and screens that flash an
+            old design or leave a player stuck. Out of scope: attacks that need a modified app, style, naming and
+            refactors. House rules a change must keep: equal scores are a DRAW (no tiebreak anywhere); Showdowns are
+            1, 3, 5 or 10 seasons; nothing may dim or cover the screen; the loading screen stays until the next screen
+            is ready.
+
+            For each finding give the file and line, what goes wrong for the players, and the smallest fix, as an inline
+            comment where possible. Then post one short summary comment; if nothing real is found, say so in one line.
+          claude_args: |
+            --model ${{ steps.plan.outputs.model }}
+            --max-turns 40
+            --allowedTools "mcp__github_inline_comment__create_inline_comment,Bash(gh pr comment:*),Bash(gh pr diff:*),Bash(gh pr view:*),Read,Grep,Glob"

The Physical Journey panel row `SEASONS n/3` counts completed seasons and ticks at 3/3 once the completed plan is recorded.
-   - Results regression probe: before either manager publishes, both devices must remain on their unpublished Review screen for at least 35 seconds. Ordinary polling must not kick either device back to result entry, and any visible publication/review error must remain visible long enough to read.
-   - After both publish, the same Results screen must expose the Shared Season Commit flow. Daniel/coordinator commits the shared snapshot, then Daniel and Nik independently acknowledge it. Canonical scoring and Shared History must appear without manually loading another capability or returning to a dead-end r9 screen.
-   - If Daniel sees `CHECK SHARED SEASON COMMIT` or `RETRY COMMIT CHECK`, press it once. This checks the existing published results without changing them. If it returns `COMMIT SHARED SEASON`, Daniel may continue. If it shows a failure code, keep both Showdowns open and save a screenshot of the code; do not restart, republish, or use the ordinary local `CONFIRM & SAVE SEASON` button.
-3. Only after the **last** season's Shared History has converged, take each device genuinely offline once and then reconnect it. Keep it offline long enough for the browser to observe the offline state. Wait until the Physical Journey panel shows OFFLINE RECOVERY complete.
-4. Reload the same tab on each device once after recovery and before Local Reconciliation or terminal closure. Wait for RELOAD to become complete. Continue the same rivalry; do not redraw or reset.
-5. After the required post-recovery reload, stay in the post-results flow and press **`PREVIEW LOCAL RECONCILIATION`** once when it appears. Wait for `PREVIEW READY`. This is the safe read-only preview; **do not apply Candidate C** from Settings/advanced recovery. The Physical Journey recorder hashes canonical local storage immediately before and immediately after this preview to prove that the preview itself is non-destructive; normal Save Library changes from legitimate setup/gameplay before this point are expected and are not treated as corruption.
-6. Reach Final Reconciliation for the last season, then complete Terminal Close. The panel must show CLOSED.
-7. Reload the same tab on both devices once more after CLOSED. The panel must show CLOSED AFTER RELOAD and no replacement session or extra season may resurrect the Showdown.
-8. Confirm the saved device/network labels, `AUTHORITY` complete, and all expected indicators. On each device press `DOWNLOAD JSON` (or `COPY EVIDENCE` if download is unavailable) and provide both sanitized exports together.
-
-A successful run therefore has at least three recorder startups on each device: initial load, the required post-recovery/pre-terminal reload, and the distinct post-CLOSED reload.
-
-## Acceptance oracle
-
-Run `npm run validate:ssjr-physical-journey -- <player-one.json> <player-two.json>`. A valid pair requires opposite manager and host/peer roles, stable manager/account/device/rivalry authority, distinct account/device fingerprints, distinct device/network evidence, one shared rivalry and correlated private session, every season of one confirmed 1/3/5/10 plan in order (plus the completed plan for multi-season runs), the ordered last-season History → offline → online → reconnect → reload → scoped Local Reconciliation storage proof → PREVIEW_READY → Final Reconciliation → Terminal Close → post-CLOSED reload path, the independent non-writing conflict-guard proof, and no Candidate C Apply observation.
-
-The recorder is acceptance-only, persists only sanitized evidence in sessionStorage, makes no recorder-owned network request, exports no raw private authority, and never applies Candidate C. The canonical-storage invariant is deliberately scoped to the read-only Local Reconciliation preview rather than the entire playable career.

+## Correction (2026-10-27, independent audit F05)
+
+The "Firestore Rules" section above says "No Rules change." That is wrong for r84 (PR #1002). The text above is kept as
+originally written; this section is the correction.
+
+What PR #1002 changed in the Rules, exactly:
+
+- `firestore.spark.rules` and `firestore.stage5c.rules` (identical in this PR): reads and the Session offer `get` now use
+  a new `livePairedRivalryMember` check (one rivalry lookup) instead of `activePairedRivalry` plus the two account
+  lookups; `currentlyEntitled` checks `signedIn()` instead of `activeActor()`; `sessionCanRead` uses
+  `livePairedRivalryMember` and `resource == null` instead of a second `exists()`.
+- `firestore.shared-setup-production.fragment.rules`: `ssjrEntitled` uses `livePairedRivalryMember`;
+  `ssjrWriteAuthorityValid` no longer looks up the two account documents or the device document on every write (two
+  lookups per write instead of five); a new `ssjrBothAccountsActive` check runs once when a Shared Setup opens.
+- `scripts/build-production-firestore-rules.mjs` (the build guard): updated to match the new Rules text.
+
+Net effect: fewer Rules lookups, but account and device liveness are now checked at Session start and Shared Setup open
+instead of on every read or write. This is a real change to the deployed Rules.
+
+Rollback: rolling the app back through the service-worker cache (r83 shell is retained) does NOT roll back the Rules.
+Firestore Rules are published separately and stay at whatever was last published. Rolling back only the app leaves r83
+code running against the r84 Rules. The coherent rollback pair is the r84 app together with the r84 generated Rules,
+i.e. the build of `scripts/build-production-firestore-rules.mjs` at r84 main commit
+`cd7df26ed57e4d9dfa40d88042d25a71fcb47a6c`. Rebuilt on 2026-10-27 at that commit, `firestore.spark.generated.rules` is
+126,689 bytes with SHA-256 `36d58d21147004f3c768cdadb219c4617f78d0c312b74207b6606844cc98b131` (the same hash at
+`5b0ab3ee`, the #1002 merge). The independent audit recorded 156,903 bytes and
+`686c87f432be7371a595ec7822ffd7f2bbc12f5ad809296abaed636785ed94b4` for the same commit; that value did not reproduce
+from this repository, so the rebuilt value above is the one to compare against until the two are reconciled. The
+pre-r84 (r83, commit `186af266`, the parent of #1002) generated Rules hash to
+`456f2b159bf3b025b374dc0c24ca7504f42ec7c4aeeff17293cb9a18b4e5d915`.

+# Career Mode Showdown — r85: marble music player, premium club crests, safer shared writes (v1.9.9 runtime r85)
+
+Application version: `v1.9.9`  
+Runtime asset revision: `1.9.9-r85`  
+Previous known-good runtime: `1.9.8-r84`
+
+r85 builds on r84. The application version moves to 1.9.9 and the runtime revision to r85.
+
+## What changed
+
+- Music: nine new songs, with Blur "Song 2" first and John Newman "Love Me Again" second. A marble player sits in the
+  middle of the desktop top bar on every screen that has the top bar; Settings has none. On phones it is the MUSIC tab
+  in the bottom bar plus Home TRACKS. The tracklist is a readable, keyboard-friendly list with the current song marked
+  (#1010, #1018).
+- Premium club crests: 98 drawn crests, with no lettering, on Club, Standings, Rivalry and Legacy (#1012).
+- Settings SEASONS LEFT TODAY starts from the trimmed season cost (#1011).
+- Season acknowledgement race: a second proving read after 400 ms, so a tap made just before the rival's acknowledgement
+  is recorded (#1009).
+- SSJR test panel (`?ssjr=1`) records the real two-phone run itself, restarts after a closed, failed run, and has
+  readable buttons (#1014, #1020).
+- Reads counter: direct gets and listeners are counted (#1016).
+- Docs: the R84 correction note, current entry points, and scoring flags that say draws (#1015).
+
+## Update safety
+
+- The shell and service worker advance to r85 in a fresh `career-mode-showdown-shell-1.9.9-r85` cache, and r84 is retained
+  for rollback. The new cache revision also stops old crests from flashing after the update.
+
+## What did not change
+
+- The scoring formula and the Remote Joining protocol stay as they were. No stored document shape changes. No tiebreak.
+- The no-flash rules, the Reus Gate, preload and decode are unchanged.
+- Firebase remains Spark-only, billing remains OFF and App Check enforcement remains OFF.
+- No saved Showdown is rewritten. Nothing in Firebase is deleted or edited.
+
+## Firestore Rules
+
+- Rules change (audit R84 F01/F02, PR #1017). Every shared write (setup, transfers, results, season commit create and
+  acknowledge) now checks the acting account is active, the acting device is registered and active, and the session
+  belongs to both members (`ssjrActorWriteAuthorityValid`). The unused `ssjrCommitShape` helper is deleted. Reads stay
+  membership-only. Cost: about 28 more billed Rules lookups per season.
+- Generated Rules at the release head: `firestore.spark.generated.rules`, 123,027 bytes, SHA-256
+  `b6a3206e0b1f4296a12450e7670feba20e5902e727aa8e996b7ef9fa6cf20f19`.
+- Rollback pair: rolling back only the app does not roll back the Rules. The r84 rollback pair is the r84 app with the
+  r84 generated Rules (SHA-256 `36d58d21147004f3c768cdadb219c4617f78d0c312b74207b6606844cc98b131`).
+
+## Checks
+
+- Filled in on the release PR from the exact release head.
+
+## SSJR status
+
+SSJR-1.1 remains frozen at `0/100`. SSJR-2.1 is at `0/100` until a validated two-device production run is recorded. No
+SSJR credit comes from this source, CI or deployment. No physical acceptance is claimed for r85.

+- SSJR-2.2 evidence rules (owner request 2026-10-26, see `authority-history/OWNER_SSJR22_SIMPLE_SSJR_TEST_2026-10-26.md`): offline, reconnect and reload checks during the game (after the career starts, before the automatic close); the close confirmed after a later reload; Local and Final Reconciliation proven on at least one device of the pair. Capabilities, weights and dependencies are unchanged from SSJR-2.1.

+# Owner authority: SSJR-2.2 simple SSJR test (2026-10-26)
+
+**Owner:** Nik (product owner), in the Team G lead thread:
+- 2026-10-26 23:20 UTC: "streamline and simplify processes while making sure we prove ssjr 100 to make gameplay officially lunch as fully playable".
+- 2026-10-26 23:46 UTC: chose "One run proves both": one 3-season real-device game proves r84 and SSJR 100.
+- 2026-10-26 23:47 UTC: "Build ssjr test a lot easier and simpler panel that would be automated".
+
+**Why the evidence rules changed.** An emulator run of the SSJR-2.1 recorder on runtime r84 could not complete. Since JOB-1038 the Showdown closes itself after the last season, so the SSJR-2.1 order (offline, reload and Local Reconciliation preview after the last season, then a manual close, then a reload) is impossible. The automatic close also races each device's Local and Final Reconciliation.
+
+**What SSJR-2.2 changes (evidence rules only).**
+- One link, `?ssjr=1`, loads only the recorder panel: no typed labels (the device label comes from browser facts; the network is one tap), no reset or check buttons.
+- The offline, reconnect (after the game held its state offline) and reload checks are made during the game, after the career starts and before the automatic close. The reload must find the same Showdown again.
+- The close is recorded live, then confirmed after a later reload by one read-only check of the signed-in account's career index and the completed-Showdown reader. The recorder still never writes to Firebase.
+- Local Reconciliation (read-only PREVIEW_READY with its unchanged storage proof) and Final Reconciliation of the last season must be proven on at least one device of the pair. A preview that a device does record must carry its storage proof. Candidate C Apply stays forbidden.
+
+**What does not change.** SSJR-2.1 capabilities, weights, dependencies, the credit script and owner attestation, the rule that only a validated two-device production pair plus Nik's attestation earns credit, and the rule that no emulator, CI, review or merge evidence earns credit.

+// r85 (Nik 2026-10-27): the soundtrack player outside Home's own strip.
+// - Desktop: a compact dock in the top bar, after the tabs, on every screen (Home too: its strip is hidden on desktop).
+// - Phone and tablet (900px and under): a sixth MUSIC tab in the tab bar, and Home's TRACKS button, open the player sheet.
+// - Both open one player panel: now playing, progress, previous / play / next / mute, and a one-column tracklist with
+//   every title and artist written in full, scrolling inside itself. It is opaque and never dims the screen behind it.
+// It drives Home's one <audio> through window.HomeSoundtrack, so a song keeps playing across screens. Nothing here touches
+// Firestore; Audius is only asked for a song after a Play tap. Looks: css/v10Shell.css (.cmsMusicDock, .cmsMusicSheet, .cmsMusicTab).
+(function(root){
+  "use strict";
+  if(!root||root.CareerModeMusicDockV10)return;
+  const PHONE_MAX=900;
+  let dock=null,sheet=null,watching=null,started=false;
+  const player=()=>root.HomeSoundtrack;
+  const setText=(el,v)=>{if(el&&el.textContent!==String(v))el.textContent=String(v);};
+  const isAudio=event=>Boolean(event.target&&event.target.classList&&event.target.classList.contains("menuAudiusAudio"));
+  const mdAudio=()=>root.document.querySelector("audio.menuAudiusAudio");
+  const mdClock=sec=>{if(!Number.isFinite(sec)||sec<0)return"-:--";const m=Math.floor(sec/60),s=Math.floor(sec%60);return`${m}:${String(s).padStart(2,"0")}`;};
+  const ICON={
+    prev:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 5h2v14H6zM20 5v14L9.5 12z"/></svg>',
+    next:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M16 5h2v14h-2zM4 5v14l10.5-7z"/></svg>',
+    play:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 4.5v15L19.5 12z"/></svg>',
+    pause:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6.5 4.5h4v15h-4zM13.5 4.5h4v15h-4z"/></svg>',
+    sound:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 9h4l5-4v14l-5-4H4z"/><path d="M16 8.5a5 5 0 0 1 0 7M18.5 6a8.5 8.5 0 0 1 0 12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>',
+    muted:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 9h4l5-4v14l-5-4H4z"/><path d="M16.5 9.5l5 5M21.5 9.5l-5 5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>',
+    list:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 6h11v2H4zM4 11h11v2H4zM4 16h7v2H4zM17 6h3v8.2a3 3 0 1 1-2-2.8V8h-1z"/></svg>',
+    close:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>',
+    disc:'<svg class="cmsMusicTabDisc" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="6.5" fill="none" stroke="#0a0b0e" stroke-opacity=".55" stroke-width=".8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3.4" fill="#0a0b0e"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="1.2"/></svg>'
+  };
+  const swapIcon=(btn,key)=>{if(btn.dataset.icon!==key){btn.dataset.icon=key;btn.innerHTML=ICON[key];}};
+
+  // A tapped song starts playing (the tap is the play gesture the browser asks for).
+  function mdPick(key){
+    const api=player();
+    api.choose(key);
+    if(!api.state().wantPlaying)api.toggle();
+  }
+  function mdStep(dir){
+    const api=player(),all=api.tracks(),i=all.findIndex(t=>t.key===api.current().key);
+    mdPick(all[(i+dir+all.length)%all.length].key);
+  }
+
+  function mdRender(){
+    const api=player(),t=api&&api.current&&api.current();
+    if(!t)return;
+    const s=api.state(),failed=Boolean(s.failed),playing=Boolean(s.playing&&s.wantPlaying);
+    const artist=failed?"STREAM UNAVAILABLE · PICK ANOTHER":t.artist;
+    if(dock){
+      setText(dock.title,t.title);setText(dock.artist,artist);
+      swapIcon(dock.toggle,s.wantPlaying?"pause":"play");
+      dock.toggle.setAttribute("aria-label",(s.wantPlaying?"Pause ":"Play ")+t.title);dock.toggle.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(Boolean(s.wantPlaying)));
+      // Mute waits for the first Play, as on Home (there is no sound to mute before it).
+      dock.mute.disabled=!s.src;swapIcon(dock.mute,s.muted?"muted":"sound");
+      dock.mute.setAttribute("aria-label",s.muted?"Unmute":"Mute");dock.mute.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(Boolean(s.muted)));
+      dock.open.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(Boolean(sheet&&!sheet.root.hidden)));
+      dock.root.dataset.playing=String(playing);dock.root.dataset.failed=String(failed);
+    }
+    if(sheet){
+      setText(sheet.title,t.title);setText(sheet.artist,artist);
+      swapIcon(sheet.play,s.wantPlaying?"pause":"play");
+      sheet.play.setAttribute("aria-label",(s.wantPlaying?"Pause ":"Play ")+t.title);
+      sheet.mute.disabled=!s.src;swapIcon(sheet.mute,s.muted?"muted":"sound");
+      sheet.mute.setAttribute("aria-label",s.muted?"Unmute":"Mute");sheet.mute.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(Boolean(s.muted)));
+      sheet.root.dataset.playing=String(playing);sheet.root.dataset.failed=String(failed);
+      // The current song: aria-current plus a visible "now playing" icon (css), never colour alone. It is also the row
+      // that Tab enters the list on (roving tabindex), unless the listener has already moved within the list.
+      const inList=sheet.list.contains(root.document.activeElement);
+      sheet.songs.forEach(b=>{
+        const on=b.dataset.soundtrackTrack===t.key;b.classList.toggle("selected",on);
+        if(on)b.setAttribute("aria-current","true");else b.removeAttribute("aria-current");
+        if(!inList)b.tabIndex=on?0:-1;
+      });
+      mdTime();
+    }
+    const tab=root.document.getElementById("cmsMusicTab");
+    if(tab){tab.dataset.playing=String(playing);tab.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(Boolean(sheet&&!sheet.root.hidden&&sheet.root.dataset.mode==="phone")));}
+    // A longer title or artist widens the dock, so the fit beside the account chips is worked out again (Codex, PR #1010).
+    mdFitTop();
+  }
+
+  function mdTime(){
+    if(!sheet||sheet.root.hidden)return;
+    const a=mdAudio(),cur=a?a.currentTime:0,dur=a?a.duration:NaN;
+    sheet.bar.style.transform=`scaleX(${Number.isFinite(dur)&&dur>0?Math.min(1,cur/dur):0})`;
+    setText(sheet.times[0],mdClock(cur||0));setText(sheet.times[1],mdClock(dur));
+  }
+
+  // ---- the player panel (phone sheet, desktop drop-down) ----
+  function mdSheetBuild(){
+    const doc=root.document,tracks=player().tracks(),el=doc.createElement("div");
+    el.id="cmsMusicSheet";el.className="cmsMusicSheet";el.setAttribute("role","dialog");el.setAttribute("aria-label","Soundtrack player");el.hidden=true;
+    el.innerHTML='<div class="cmsMusicSheetNow"><span class="cmsMusicSheetDisc" aria-hidden="true"><i></i></span>'+
+      '<div class="cmsMusicSheetText"><span>NOW PLAYING</span><strong></strong><small></small></div>'+
+      `<button class="cmsMusicSheetClose" type="button" aria-label="Close soundtrack player">${ICON.close}</button></div>`+
+      '<div class="cmsMusicSheetTime"><span class="cmsMusicSheetBar" aria-hidden="true"><i></i></span><small>0:00</small><small>-:--</small></div>'+
+      '<div class="cmsMusicSheetControls">'+
+      '<button class="cmsMusicSheetMute" type="button" aria-label="Mute" disabled></button>'+
+      `<button class="cmsMusicSheetPrev" type="button" aria-label="Previous song">${ICON.prev}</button>`+
+      '<button class="cmsMusicSheetPlay" type="button" aria-label="Play"></button>'+
+      `<button class="cmsMusicSheetNext" type="button" aria-label="Next song">${ICON.next}</button>`+
+      '<span class="cmsMusicSheetEq" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i></span></div>'+
+      `<div class="cmsMusicSheetHead" aria-hidden="true"><strong>TRACKLIST</strong><small>${tracks.length} SONGS</small></div>`+
+      `<ul class="cmsMusicSheetList" role="list" aria-label="Tracklist, ${tracks.length} songs. Choose a song to play it"></ul>`;
+    const list=el.querySelector(".cmsMusicSheetList");
+    tracks.forEach((t,i)=>{
+      const li=doc.createElement("li"),b=doc.createElement("button");
+      b.type="button";b.className="cmsMusicSheetSong";b.dataset.soundtrackTrack=t.key;b.tabIndex=-1;
+      b.innerHTML='<em aria-hidden="true"></em><span><strong></strong><small></small></span><i class="cmsMusicSheetSongEq" aria-hidden="true"><b></b><b></b><b></b></i>';
+      b.querySelector("em").textContent=String(i+1).padStart(2,"0");
+      b.querySelector("strong").textContent=t.title;
+      b.querySelector("small").textContent=t.artist;
+      li.appendChild(b);list.appendChild(li);
+    });
+    // A clear layer that closes the panel on an outside tap. It has no colour: the screen behind is never dimmed.
+    const shield=doc.createElement("div");shield.className="cmsMusicSheetShield";shield.hidden=true;shield.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
+    doc.body.append(shield,el);
+    const q=c=>el.querySelector(c);
+    sheet={root:el,shield,list,title:q(".cmsMusicSheetText strong"),artist:q(".cmsMusicSheetText small"),play:q(".cmsMusicSheetPlay"),mute:q(".cmsMusicSheetMute"),
+      bar:q(".cmsMusicSheetBar i"),times:el.querySelectorAll(".cmsMusicSheetTime small"),songs:Array.from(list.querySelectorAll(".cmsMusicSheetSong")),close:q(".cmsMusicSheetClose"),opener:null};
+    sheet.play.addEventListener("click",()=>player().toggle());
+    sheet.mute.addEventListener("click",()=>player().mute());
+    q(".cmsMusicSheetPrev").addEventListener("click",()=>mdStep(-1));
+    q(".cmsMusicSheetNext").addEventListener("click",()=>mdStep(1));
+    sheet.close.addEventListener("click",()=>mdSheetClose());
+    shield.addEventListener("click",()=>mdSheetClose());
+    list.addEventListener("click",event=>{
+      const b=event.target instanceof root.Element?event.target.closest("[data-soundtrack-track]"):null;
+      if(b)mdPick(b.dataset.soundtrackTrack);
+    });
+    // Keyboard: Tab enters the list once (on the current song), the arrow keys, Home and End move between songs, and
+    // Enter or Space plays the focused one (it is a real button).
+    list.addEventListener("focusin",event=>{
+      const b=event.target;
+      if(sheet.songs.includes(b))sheet.songs.forEach(x=>{x.tabIndex=x===b?0:-1;});
+    });
+    list.addEventListener("keydown",event=>{
+      const i=sheet.songs.indexOf(event.target);
+      if(i<0||event.altKey||event.ctrlKey||event.metaKey)return;
+      const last=sheet.songs.length-1;
+      const to={ArrowDown:Math.min(last,i+1),ArrowUp:Math.max(0,i-1),Home:0,End:last,PageDown:Math.min(last,i+5),PageUp:Math.max(0,i-5)}[event.key];
+      if(to===undefined)return;
+      event.preventDefault();
+      sheet.songs[to].focus();
+    });
+    return sheet;
+  }
+
+  // Phone: the sheet ends just above the tab bar, so the tabs stay usable. Desktop: it drops from the dock, under the top bar.
+  function mdSheetPlace(){
+    if(!sheet)return;
+    const r=sheet.root.style,doc=root.document;
+    if(root.innerWidth>PHONE_MAX){
+      const bar=doc.querySelector(".sd-nav-top"),bb=bar&&bar.getBoundingClientRect(),db=dock&&dock.root.getBoundingClientRect();
+      const top=Math.round((bb&&bb.height?bb.bottom:56)+8),width=Math.min(420,root.innerWidth-24);
+      const left=Math.round(Math.max(12,Math.min(db&&db.width?db.left:root.innerWidth-width-12,root.innerWidth-width-12)));
+      sheet.root.dataset.mode="desktop";r.setProperty("--cms-sheet-top",`${top}px`);r.setProperty("--cms-sheet-left",`${left}px`);r.setProperty("--cms-sheet-width",`${width}px`);
+      sheet.shield.style.setProperty("--cms-sheet-bottom","0px");
+      return;
+    }
+    const nav=doc.querySelector(".sd-nav-bottom"),box=nav&&nav.getBoundingClientRect();
+    const bottom=box&&box.height>2&&box.top<root.innerHeight?Math.round(Math.max(0,root.innerHeight-box.top)):0;
+    sheet.root.dataset.mode="phone";r.setProperty("--cms-sheet-bottom",`${bottom}px`);
+    sheet.shield.style.setProperty("--cms-sheet-bottom",`${bottom}px`);
+  }
+
+  function mdSheetOpen(opener){
+    if(!sheet&&!mdSheetBuild())return;
+    sheet.opener=opener||null;
+    mdSheetPlace();
+    sheet.shield.hidden=false;sheet.root.hidden=false;
+    mdRender();
+    // The current song is scrolled to the middle of the list (instantly, so reduced motion is respected).
+    const on=sheet.list.querySelector('[aria-current="true"]');
+    if(on)sheet.list.scrollTop=Math.max(0,on.offsetTop-sheet.list.clientHeight/2+on.offsetHeight/2);
+    root.requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(sheet&&!sheet.root.hidden)sheet.root.dataset.open="true";});
+    sheet.close.focus({preventScroll:true});
+  }
+
+  function mdSheetClose(){
+    if(!sheet||sheet.root.hidden)return;
+    sheet.root.hidden=true;sheet.shield.hidden=true;delete sheet.root.dataset.open;
+    const back=sheet.opener;sheet.opener=null;
+    if(back&&back.isConnected&&back.focus)back.focus({preventScroll:true});
+    mdRender();
+  }
+  const mdSheetToggle=opener=>{if(sheet&&!sheet.root.hidden)mdSheetClose();else mdSheetOpen(opener);};
+
+  // ---- desktop dock ----
+  function mdBuildDock(host,before){
+    const doc=root.document,el=doc.createElement("div");
+    el.id="cmsMusicDockTop";el.className="cmsMusicDock";el.setAttribute("role","group");el.setAttribute("aria-label","Soundtrack");
+    el.innerHTML='<span class="cmsMusicDockDisc" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="cmsMusicDockText"><strong></strong><small></small></span>'+
+      '<button class="cmsMusicDockPlay" type="button" aria-label="Play"></button>'+
+      '<button class="cmsMusicDockMute" type="button" aria-label="Mute" disabled></button>'+
+      `<button class="cmsMusicDockOpen" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="cmsMusicSheet">${ICON.list}<span>TRACKS</span></button>`;
+    host.insertBefore(el,before||null);
+    dock={root:el,title:el.querySelector(".cmsMusicDockText strong"),artist:el.querySelector(".cmsMusicDockText small"),
+      toggle:el.querySelector(".cmsMusicDockPlay"),mute:el.querySelector(".cmsMusicDockMute"),open:el.querySelector(".cmsMusicDockOpen")};
+    dock.toggle.addEventListener("click",()=>player().toggle());
+    dock.mute.addEventListener("click",()=>player().mute());
+    dock.open.addEventListener("click",()=>mdSheetToggle(dock.open));
+    return dock;
+  }
+
+  // The account chips sit at the right of the desktop bar (css/v10Shell.css, fixed). When the bar is too narrow for the
+  // whole dock beside them, the artist line goes first, then the title, so the dock never touches a chip.
+  function mdChipsLeft(bar){
+    let left=Infinity;
+    for(const id of ["onlinePlayerIdentityBadge","seasonIndicator"]){
+      const el=root.document.getElementById(id),b=el&&el.getBoundingClientRect();
+      if(b&&b.width>2&&b.height>2&&b.top<bar.bottom&&b.bottom>bar.top)left=Math.min(left,b.left);
+    }
+    const capsule=root.document.querySelector(".sd-nav-top .sd-nav-capsule"),c=capsule&&capsule.getBoundingClientRect();
+    if(c&&c.width)left=Math.min(left,c.left);
+    return left;
+  }
+  // The dock sits in the middle of the bar: the page's centre when that leaves a clear gap after the tabs, otherwise the
+  // centre of the free space between the tabs and the account chips. When even that is too tight it shrinks (artist
+  // line first, then the title) and, as a last step, steps out of the bar, so it never touches a tab or a chip.
+  function mdFitTop(){
+    if(!dock)return;
+    const el=dock.root,prev=el.previousElementSibling;
+    for(const mode of ["full","title","icon"]){
+      el.dataset.fit=mode;el.style.marginLeft="";
+      const box=el.getBoundingClientRect();
+      if(!box.width)return;
+      const start=prev?prev.getBoundingClientRect().right:el.parentNode.getBoundingClientRect().left;
+      const end=mdChipsLeft(el.parentNode.getBoundingClientRect());
+      const w=box.width,inFlow=box.left;
+      let left=root.innerWidth/2-w/2;
+      if(left<start+48)left=((start+(Number.isFinite(end)?end:root.innerWidth))/2)-w/2;
+      left=Math.max(left,start+18);
+      if(left+w+16<=end){el.style.marginLeft=`${Math.round(left-inFlow+parseFloat(root.getComputedStyle(el).marginLeft||0))}px`;return;}
+    }
+    el.style.marginLeft="";el.dataset.fit="none";
+  }
+
+  // ---- phone MUSIC tab: the sixth tab of the tab bar. The bar keeps its height, so nothing on any screen moves. ----
+  function mdTabMount(){
+    const doc=root.document,nav=doc.querySelector(".sd-nav-bottom");
+    if(!nav||nav.querySelector(".cmsMusicTab"))return;
+    const b=doc.createElement("button");
+    b.type="button";b.id="cmsMusicTab";b.className="sd-nav-tab cmsMusicTab";b.setAttribute("aria-label","Music player");b.setAttribute("aria-controls","cmsMusicSheet");b.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
+    b.innerHTML=`${ICON.disc}<span>MUSIC</span>`;
+    b.addEventListener("click",()=>mdSheetToggle(b));
+    nav.appendChild(b);
+    mdRender();
+  }
+
+  // Home's TRACKS button is a label for a hidden checkbox (Team V markup). Under 900px the checkbox is turned straight back
+  // off in the same task, before the old grid can paint, and the sheet opens instead.
+  function mdHomeHook(){
+    const doc=root.document,box=doc.getElementById("phoneTrackSheetToggle");
+    if(!box||box.dataset.cmsSheet)return;
+    box.dataset.cmsSheet="true";
+    box.addEventListener("change",()=>{
+      if(!box.checked||root.innerWidth>PHONE_MAX)return;
+      box.checked=false;
+      mdSheetOpen(doc.querySelector(".menuMusicTile .phoneTrackSheetOpen")||box);
+    });
+  }
+
+  function mdMount(){
+    const doc=root.document,top=doc.querySelector(".sd-nav-top");
+    if(top&&!doc.getElementById("cmsMusicDockTop")){mdBuildDock(top,top.querySelector(".sd-nav-capsule"));mdRender();}
+    mdTabMount();
+    return Boolean(top);
+  }
+
+  function mdStart(){
+    const api=player(),doc=root.document;
+    mdHomeHook();
+    if(started||!api||typeof api.subscribe!=="function"||!api.current())return false;
+    started=true;
+    api.subscribe(mdRender);
+    mdMount();
+    // The bars can be built (or rebuilt) after Home: mount into them as they appear.
+    if(typeof root.MutationObserver==="function"){
+      watching=new root.MutationObserver(mdMount);
+      watching.observe(doc.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
+      new root.MutationObserver(()=>{mdSheetClose();root.requestAnimationFrame(mdFitTop);}).observe(doc.documentElement,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:["data-v10-screen","data-nav"]});
+    }
+    // Escape closes the player first (it is the top layer) and hands focus back to the button that opened it; a dialog
+    // underneath (Settings) stays open until a second Escape.
+    doc.addEventListener("keydown",event=>{
+      if(event.key!=="Escape"||!sheet||sheet.root.hidden)return;
+      event.stopPropagation();mdSheetClose();
+    },true);
+    doc.addEventListener("timeupdate",event=>{if(isAudio(event))mdTime();},true);
+    doc.addEventListener("durationchange",event=>{if(isAudio(event))mdTime();},true);
+    let wasPhone=root.innerWidth<=PHONE_MAX;
+    root.addEventListener("resize",()=>{
+      const phone=root.innerWidth<=PHONE_MAX;
+      if(phone!==wasPhone){wasPhone=phone;mdSheetClose();}else mdSheetPlace();
+      mdFitTop();
+    });
+    if(typeof root.ResizeObserver==="function"){const chips=doc.getElementById("topHeader");if(chips)new root.ResizeObserver(mdFitTop).observe(chips);}
+    return true;

+  // r85 (audit F04): ONE read meter. Before, only this layer's transactions and listener deliveries were counted, while the
+  // career marker, the career index, the finished-Showdown reader and the Season Final history called getDoc taken straight
+  // from the SDK: a cold career visit of 100 finished Showdowns made 601 gets and left readsToday at 0. Now every route ends
+  // in fsbMeterUnits/fsbMeterFailureUnits -> fsbCountRead:
+  //  - transactions run through this layer: one per document the server returns to the transaction (unchanged);
+  //  - direct getDoc/getDocs/getDocFromServer/getDocsFromServer: services(...) meters them when the runtime hands them out,
+  //    and every module that takes them from the Firestore module itself passes them through meterSdk(...) first;
+  //  - listeners: this layer's own (wait point, context) and the unowned ones (season-commit wait context, Home JOIN note,
+  //    which pass their onSnapshot through meterSdk) count each delivery here.
+  // What is counted is REQUESTED SERVER DOCUMENT READS, the unit of the free daily limit:
+  //  - a document from the server: 1 (a missing document is still one read);
+  //  - a query answer from the server: 1 per returned document, at least 1 (an empty answer is billed one read); a later
+  //    delivery of a query listener: 1 per changed document;
+  //  - a snapshot served from the local cache (metadata.fromCache) or a listener's echo of this page's own pending write: 0;
+  //  - a request the server answered with an error (denied by Rules, invalid, not found...): 1, as Firestore bills it; one that
+  //    never got an answer (offline/unavailable, cancelled, deadline, quota) or failed in the page before the SDK sent it: 0.
+  // Each function is metered once (fsbMetered), so an SDK object handed through several screens never counts a read twice.
+  // Direct gets count toward today's total but not the brake window, so a long cold career visit never slows the live checks.
+  // The count stays a per-device estimate: Rules lookups (the 2.8 factor), the partner's phone and other devices are not seen.
+  const FSB_ANSWERED_ERRORS=new Set(["permission-denied","unauthenticated","invalid-argument","not-found","failed-precondition","out-of-range","already-exists","internal","data-loss","unimplemented"]);

+  function fsbMeterUnits(snapshot,listener=false,later=false){
+    if(!snapshot||typeof snapshot!=="object")return 0;
+    const meta=snapshot.metadata;
+    if(meta&&meta.fromCache===true)return 0;
+    if(listener&&meta&&meta.hasPendingWrites===true)return 0;
+    if(Array.isArray(snapshot.docs)){
+      if(later&&typeof snapshot.docChanges==="function"){try{return snapshot.docChanges().length;}catch(_error){return 0;}}
+      return Math.max(1,snapshot.docs.length);
+    }
+    return 1;
+  }
+  function fsbMeterFailureUnits(error){
+    if(!error||typeof error!=="object"||error.quotaPaused)return 0;
+    return FSB_ANSWERED_ERRORS.has(String(error.code||"").toLowerCase().replace(/^firestore\//,"").replace(/_/g,"-"))?1:0;
+  }
+  function fsbMeterGet(get){
+    if(typeof get!=="function"||fsbMetered.has(get))return get;
+    const metered=function(...args){
+      let pending;

+      return Promise.resolve(pending).then(snapshot=>{const units=fsbMeterUnits(snapshot);fsbStats.directReads+=units;fsbCountRead(units,false);return snapshot;},error=>{fsbStats.directFailures+=1;fsbCountRead(fsbMeterFailureUnits(error),false);throw error;});
+    };
+    fsbMetered.add(metered);

+  // onSnapshot(ref, [options], observer | onNext, [onError], [onCompletion]): the callbacks are wrapped, the call is unchanged.
+  function fsbMeterListen(listen){

+    const metered=function(ref,...rest){
+      let served=false;
+      const next=fn=>function(snapshot){const units=fsbMeterUnits(snapshot,true,served);if(units||snapshot&&snapshot.metadata&&snapshot.metadata.fromCache===false)served=true;fsbCountRead(units);return fn.apply(this,arguments);};
+      const fail=fn=>function(error){fsbCountRead(fsbMeterFailureUnits(error));return fn.apply(this,arguments);};
+      let functions=0;
+      const args=rest.map(arg=>{

+"use strict";
+// R85 (#1009) Season Commit acknowledgement: second proving read after a short pause.
+//
+// When an ACKNOWLEDGE write is refused with permission-denied, js/sparkSharedSeasonCommit.js remaps it to the retryable
+// SEASON_COMMIT_STALE_BASE_REVISION only after a fresh authority-checked read proves the stored commit advanced past the
+// revision the write read. R85 adds: if that first proving read does not show the advance (still the old revision, or the
+// read itself failed), wait 400 ms and do exactly one second proving read before calling the denial unproven.
+//
+// This contract drives that new branch deterministically (node only, no emulator, no real time): a fake Firestore that
+// denies like the Rules, and a controlled clock that replaces the provider's 400 ms setTimeout so the rival's write can be
+// placed exactly inside the pause. The production adapter's bounded acknowledgement loop (psscWrite in
+// js/productionSharedSeasonCommit.js) is replayed step for step and pinned to the adapter's source text, and every case
+// asserts the exact number of write attempts, proving reads, adapter reads and pauses, in both manager orders.
+const assert=require("node:assert/strict");
+const fs=require("node:fs");
+const path=require("node:path");
+const {webcrypto}=require("node:crypto");
+const Results=require("../../js/sharedSeasonResults.js");
+const Provider=require("../../js/sparkSharedSeasonCommit.js");
+
+const rivalryId="pair_"+("6".repeat(64));
+const sessionId="session_"+("c".repeat(64));
+const DEVICE={playerOne:"device_"+("1".repeat(32)),playerTwo:"device_"+("2".repeat(32))};
+const UID={playerOne:"manager_daniel",playerTwo:"manager_nik"};
+const resultOp=n=>`season_result_op_${Number(n).toString(16).padStart(32,"0")}`;
+const commitOp=n=>`season_commit_op_${Number(n).toString(16).padStart(32,"0")}`;
+const hash=n=>`sha256:${Number(n).toString(16).padStart(64,"0")}`;
+const ts=millis=>({toMillis:()=>millis});
+const p1={leaguePosition:1,leaguePoints:87,leagueGoals:93,domesticCup:false,championsLeague:true,topScorer:true,topAssist:false};
+const p2={leaguePosition:2,leaguePoints:84,leagueGoals:101,domesticCup:true,championsLeague:false,topScorer:false,topAssist:true};
+const ORDERS=[["playerTwo","playerOne"],["playerOne","playerTwo"]];
+let checks=0;const ok=label=>{checks+=1;console.log(`ok ${checks} ${label}`);};
+let opSeq=100;const nextOp=()=>commitOp(++opSeq);
+
+function clone(value){if(value===undefined)return undefined;const text=JSON.stringify(value,(_key,item)=>item&&typeof item.toMillis==="function"?{__millis:item.toMillis()}:item);return JSON.parse(text,(_key,item)=>item&&Number.isFinite(item.__millis)?ts(item.__millis):item);}
+function codeError(code,message){const error=new Error(message||code);error.code=code;return error;}
+const denied=()=>codeError("permission-denied","Missing or insufficient permissions.");
+
+async function createHarness(){
+  const store=new Map(),versions=new Map();let clock=0;
+  const key=(...parts)=>parts.join("/");
+  const put=(p,value)=>{store.set(p,clone(value));versions.set(p,++clock);};
+  for(const role of ["playerOne","playerTwo"]){
+    put(key("accounts",UID[role]),{objectType:"account",objectId:UID[role],lifecycleState:"live",data:{status:"active"}});
+    put(key("accounts",UID[role],"devices",DEVICE[role]),{objectType:"device",objectId:DEVICE[role],lifecycleState:"live",data:{deviceId:DEVICE[role],state:"active"}});
+  }
+  put(key("rivalries",rivalryId),{objectType:"rivalry",objectId:rivalryId,lifecycleState:"live",data:{connectionState:"active",authorizedAccountIds:[UID.playerOne,UID.playerTwo],managerSlots:[{slotId:"playerOne",accountId:UID.playerOne,profileId:"profile_"+("1".repeat(24)),saveId:"save_"+("3".repeat(24)),entitlementState:"active"},{slotId:"playerTwo",accountId:UID.playerTwo,profileId:"profile_"+("2".repeat(24)),saveId:"save_"+("4".repeat(24)),entitlementState:"active"}]}});
+  put(key("rivalries",rivalryId,"sessions",sessionId),{objectType:"session",objectId:sessionId,lifecycleState:"live",data:{rivalryId,state:"active",hostAccountId:UID.playerOne,memberAccountIds:[UID.playerOne,UID.playerTwo],expiresAt:ts(9_000_000)}});
+  const setup={schemaVersion:1,objectType:"sharedSetupLedger",rivalryId,revision:6,phase:"SHOWDOWN_CONFIRMED",coordinatorRole:"playerOne",operationIds:[1,2,3,4,5,6].map(n=>"setup_op_"+n.toString(16).padStart(32,"0")),operationTypes:["open","commit-league","commit-clubs","commit-length","confirm","confirm"],baseRevisions:[0,1,2,3,4,5],actorRoles:["playerOne","playerOne","playerOne","playerOne","playerOne","playerTwo"],totalSeasons:3,confirmedRoles:["playerOne","playerTwo"],activeSessionId:sessionId,updatedAt:ts(1_000_000),updatedByDeviceId:DEVICE.playerTwo};
+  put(key("rivalries",rivalryId,"sharedSetup","authoritative"),setup);
+  const protocol=await Results.createProtocol({teamCount:20,cryptoImpl:webcrypto});
+  const careerStart={phase:"CAREER_START_READY",revision:2,acknowledgedRoles:["playerOne","playerTwo"]},transferChallenge={phase:"COMPLETED",seasonNumber:1,revision:7};
+  let ready=(await protocol.apply({state:null,setup,careerStart,transferChallenge,seasonNumber:1,actorRole:"playerOne",command:{type:"publish-result",operationId:resultOp(1),baseRevision:0,result:p1}})).state;
+  ready=(await protocol.apply({state:ready,setup,careerStart,transferChallenge,seasonNumber:1,actorRole:"playerTwo",command:{type:"publish-result",operationId:resultOp(2),baseRevision:1,result:p2}})).state;
+  const publicPath=key("rivalries",rivalryId,"seasonResults","season_1"),commitPath=key("rivalries",rivalryId,"seasonCommits","season_1");
+  put(publicPath,{schemaVersion:1,objectType:"sharedSeasonResults",rivalryId,seasonNumber:1,runtimeRevision:"1.9.1-r9",phase:"RESULTS_READY",revision:2,publishedRoles:[...ready.publishedRoles],operationIds:ready.receipts.map(r=>r.operationId),operationHashes:[hash(10),hash(11)],baseRevisions:ready.receipts.map(r=>r.baseRevision),actorRoles:ready.receipts.map(r=>r.actorRole)});
+  for(const role of ["playerOne","playerTwo"]){const receipt=ready.receipts.find(r=>r.actorRole===role);put(key("rivalries",rivalryId,"seasonResults","season_1","roles",role),{schemaVersion:1,objectType:"sharedSeasonResultRole",rivalryId,seasonNumber:1,managerRole:role,result:ready.results[role],operationId:receipt.operationId,commandHash:receipt.commandHash});}
+
+  // Per-manager counters. Inside one provider write call (h.write), the first transaction is the write attempt and every
+  // later transaction is a provider-internal proving read; transactions outside a write call are adapter reads (h.read).
+  const counts={playerOne:{writes:0,proofReads:0,adapterReads:0,committed:0},playerTwo:{writes:0,proofReads:0,adapterReads:0,committed:0}};
+  const inWrite={playerOne:null,playerTwo:null};
+  const roleOf=uid=>uid===UID.playerOne?"playerOne":uid===UID.playerTwo?"playerTwo":null;
+  // hooks:
+  //  afterCommitRead(role)  runs once, after `role`'s write transaction reads the commit document (to interleave a rival);
+  //  denyNextWrite[role]    deny `role`'s next commit unconditionally (the Rules saw a change the client cannot yet see);
+  //  failProofRead[role]    Set of proving-read ordinals (1-based, within the current write call) that fail with `unavailable`;
+  //  lostResponse[role]     apply `role`'s next commit on the server, then throw this error code to the client.
+  const hooks={afterCommitRead:null,denyNextWrite:{},failProofRead:{playerOne:new Set(),playerTwo:new Set()},lostResponse:{}};
+  const sdk={doc:(_db,...parts)=>key(...parts),serverTimestamp:()=>ts(2_000_000),runTransaction:async(_db,callback)=>{
+    const pending=[],seen=new Map();let role=null,kind=null;
+    const tx={get:async ref=>{
+      if(role===null){
+        const match=/^accounts\/([^/]+)$/.exec(ref);role=match?roleOf(match[1]):null;assert.ok(role,`first transactional read must identify the manager, got ${ref}`);
+        if(inWrite[role]){if(inWrite[role].first){inWrite[role].first=false;kind="write";counts[role].writes+=1;}else{kind="proof";counts[role].proofReads+=1;inWrite[role].proofs+=1;if(hooks.failProofRead[role].has(inWrite[role].proofs))throw codeError("unavailable","The service is currently unavailable.");}}
+        else{kind="adapter";counts[role].adapterReads+=1;}
+      }
+      seen.set(ref,versions.get(ref)||0);const present=store.has(ref),value=clone(store.get(ref));
+      if(ref===commitPath&&kind==="write"&&hooks.afterCommitRead&&hooks.afterCommitRead.role===role){const hook=hooks.afterCommitRead;hooks.afterCommitRead=null;await hook.run();}
+      return {exists:()=>present,data:()=>clone(value)};
+    },set:(ref,value)=>pending.push([ref,clone(value)])};
+    const result=await callback(tx);
+    if(pending.length){
+      assert.equal(kind,"write","only a write call may queue a write");
+      if(hooks.denyNextWrite[role]){hooks.denyNextWrite[role]=false;throw denied();}
+      // Rules: a revoked actor device can never write.
+      if(store.get(key("accounts",UID[role],"devices",DEVICE[role]))?.data?.state!=="active")throw denied();
+      // Rules see the stored document at commit time: a write from a superseded revision is denied (not aborted).
+      for(const [ref] of pending)if((versions.get(ref)||0)!==seen.get(ref))throw denied();
+      for(const [ref,value] of pending)put(ref,value);
+      counts[role].committed+=1;
+      if(hooks.lostResponse[role]){const code=hooks.lostResponse[role];hooks.lostResponse[role]=null;throw codeError(code,"response lost after the server applied the write");}
+    }
+    return result;
+  }};
+  const options=role=>({user:{uid:UID[role]},firestore:{},firebaseSdk:sdk,rivalryId,sessionId,deviceId:DEVICE[role],seasonNumber:1,cryptoImpl:webcrypto,nowEpochMs:2_000_000});
+  const write=async(method,role,operationId,baseRevision)=>{const outer=inWrite[role];inWrite[role]={first:true,proofs:0};try{return await Provider[method]({...options(role),operationId,baseRevision});}finally{inWrite[role]=outer;}};
+  const read=role=>Provider.read(options(role));
+  const revoke=role=>{store.get(key("accounts",UID[role],"devices",DEVICE[role])).data.state="revoked";versions.set(key("accounts",UID[role],"devices",DEVICE[role]),++clock);};
+  const reset=()=>{for(const role of ["playerOne","playerTwo"])counts[role]={writes:0,proofReads:0,adapterReads:0,committed:0};};
+  return {store,commitPath,hooks,counts,write,read,revoke,reset};
+}
+async function committed(){
+  const h=await createHarness();
+  const commit=await h.write("commitSeason","playerOne",nextOp(),0);
+  assert.equal(commit.ok,true,JSON.stringify(commit));assert.equal(commit.revision,1);
+  h.reset();return h;
+}
+
+// Controlled clock: every setTimeout the provider schedules is recorded and released at once (no real time passes);
+// onPause runs inside the pause, before the provider's second proving read.
+async function withClock(onPause,run){
+  const real=globalThis.setTimeout,pauses=[];let pauseError=null;
+  globalThis.setTimeout=(fn,ms,...args)=>{pauses.push(ms);Promise.resolve().then(()=>onPause?onPause(pauses.length,ms):undefined).catch(error=>{pauseError=error;}).then(()=>fn(...args));return {controlled:true};};
+  const started=process.hrtime.bigint();
+  try{const value=await run();if(pauseError)throw pauseError;return {value,pauses,elapsedMs:Number(process.hrtime.bigint()-started)/1e6};}
+  finally{globalThis.setTimeout=real;}
+}
+
+// Step-for-step replay of psscWrite("acknowledge") in js/productionSharedSeasonCommit.js (pinned to its source in A0):
+// read; if not yet acknowledged, up to two attempts with one operationId; on STALE at attempt 0 re-read, stop if
+// satisfied, else retry from the fresh revision; any other failure throws (shown to the manager as an error).
+const satisfied=current=>Boolean(current.ownAcknowledged||current.phase==="ACKNOWLEDGED");
+async function adapterAcknowledge(h,role,operationId=nextOp()){
+  let current=await h.read(role);if(!current.ok)throw codeError(current.code);
+  if(satisfied(current))return {acknowledged:true,attempts:0};
+  for(let attempt=0;attempt<2;attempt+=1){
+    const baseRevision=Number(current.revision||0);
+    const result=await h.write("acknowledgeSeason",role,operationId,baseRevision);
+    if(result&&result.ok===true)return {acknowledged:true,attempts:attempt+1,result};
+    if(result?.code==="SEASON_COMMIT_STALE_BASE_REVISION"&&attempt===0){current=await h.read(role);if(!current.ok)throw codeError(current.code);if(satisfied(current))return {acknowledged:true,attempts:1,viaRead:true};continue;}
+    throw codeError(result?.code||"SEASON_COMMIT_PROVIDER_FAILED");
+  }
+  return {acknowledged:false,attempts:2};
+}
+function ceilings(h,role,expected,label){assert.deepEqual({...h.counts[role]},expected,`${label}: exact ceilings for ${role}`);}
+
+(async()=>{
+  // A0 the replay above is the adapter's real loop, and the provider's pause is exactly one 400 ms wait between two reads.
+  {
+    const adapter=fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,"../../js/productionSharedSeasonCommit.js"),"utf8");
+    const loop=/async function psscWrite\(kind,ctx,request,current\)\{[\s\S]*?for\(let attempt=0;attempt<2;attempt\+=1\)\{[\s\S]*?if\(result&&result\.ok===true\)return true;\s*if\(result\?\.code==="SEASON_COMMIT_STALE_BASE_REVISION"&&attempt===0\)\{current=psscResultError\(await provider\.read\(ctx\.options\)\);if\(psscSatisfied\(kind,current\)\)return true;continue;\}\s*psscResultError\(result,[^\n]*\n\s*\}\s*return false;\s*\}/;
+    assert.match(adapter,loop,"adapter keeps one operationId, two attempts, one STALE re-read and throws on any other failure");
+    assert.match(adapter,/function psscSatisfied\(kind,current\)\{if\(kind==="commit"\)return Boolean\(current\.committed\);return Boolean\(current\.ownAcknowledged\|\|current\.phase==="ACKNOWLEDGED"\);\}/);
+    const provider=fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname,"../../js/sparkSharedSeasonCommit.js"),"utf8");
+    const proof=/async function scpConcurrentAdvance\(error,seenRevision,options\)\{([\s\S]*?)\n  \}/.exec(provider);
+    assert.ok(proof,"scpConcurrentAdvance present");
+    assert.equal((proof[1].match(/await scpRead\(options\)/g)||[]).length,2,"exactly two proving reads");
+    assert.equal((proof[1].match(/setTimeout\(resolve,400\)/g)||[]).length,1,"exactly one 400 ms pause");
+    assert.match(proof[1],/fresh\.revision>seenRevision/,"proof still requires a strictly newer stored revision");
+    ok("A0 adapter loop replay and provider proof shape (two reads, one 400 ms pause, strict advance) are pinned to source");
+  }
+
+  for(const [actor,rival] of ORDERS){
+    const tag=`[${actor} acknowledges, ${rival} is the rival]`;
+
+    // A1 first proving read sees the advance at once: no pause, one proving read (pre-R85 path unchanged).
+    {
+      const h=await committed();let rivalResult=null;
+      h.hooks.afterCommitRead={role:actor,run:async()=>{rivalResult=await h.write("acknowledgeSeason",rival,nextOp(),1);}};
+      const {value,pauses}=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor));
+      assert.equal(rivalResult?.ok,true,JSON.stringify(rivalResult));
+      assert.equal(value.acknowledged,true);assert.equal(value.attempts,2);assert.deepEqual(pauses,[]);
+      assert.equal(h.store.get(h.commitPath).phase,"ACKNOWLEDGED");assert.deepEqual(h.store.get(h.commitPath).acknowledgedRoles,[rival,actor]);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:2,proofReads:1,adapterReads:2,committed:1},"A1");ceilings(h,rival,{writes:1,proofReads:0,adapterReads:0,committed:1},"A1");
+      ok(`A1 ${tag} advance visible on the first proving read: STALE with no pause, one retry reaches ACKNOWLEDGED`);
+    }
+
+    // A2 the new branch: first proving read still shows revision 1, the rival's write lands during the 400 ms pause,
+    // the second proving read proves the advance, and the adapter's one bounded retry converges.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();let rivalResult=null,storeAtPause=null;
+      h.hooks.denyNextWrite[actor]=true;
+      const {value,pauses,elapsedMs}=await withClock(async()=>{storeAtPause=h.store.get(h.commitPath).revision;rivalResult=await h.write("acknowledgeSeason",rival,nextOp(),1);},()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor));
+      assert.equal(storeAtPause,1,"the first proving read ran against an unadvanced commit");
+      assert.equal(rivalResult?.ok,true,JSON.stringify(rivalResult));assert.equal(rivalResult.revision,2);
+      assert.deepEqual(pauses,[400],"exactly one 400 ms pause, on the controlled clock");
+      assert.ok(elapsedMs<5000,"no real 400 ms timer gates the test");
+      assert.equal(value.acknowledged,true);assert.equal(value.attempts,2);assert.equal(value.result.revision,3);assert.equal(value.result.phase,"ACKNOWLEDGED");
+      assert.deepEqual(h.store.get(h.commitPath).acknowledgedRoles,[rival,actor]);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:2,proofReads:2,adapterReads:2,committed:1},"A2");ceilings(h,rival,{writes:1,proofReads:0,adapterReads:0,committed:1},"A2");
+      ok(`A2 ${tag} first proving read unadvanced, rival lands during the pause, second read proves it, one bounded retry converges`);
+    }
+
+    // A3 first proving read fails (unavailable); the rival had already advanced; the second read proves it.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();
+      h.hooks.afterCommitRead={role:actor,run:async()=>{const r=await h.write("acknowledgeSeason",rival,nextOp(),1);assert.equal(r.ok,true);}};
+      h.hooks.failProofRead[actor]=new Set([1]);
+      const {value,pauses}=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor));
+      assert.deepEqual(pauses,[400]);assert.equal(value.acknowledged,true);assert.equal(value.attempts,2);
+      assert.equal(h.store.get(h.commitPath).phase,"ACKNOWLEDGED");
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:2,proofReads:2,adapterReads:2,committed:1},"A3");
+      ok(`A3 ${tag} first proving read unavailable, second read proves the advance, one bounded retry converges`);
+    }
+
+    // A3b first proving read fails AND the rival only lands during the pause.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();h.hooks.denyNextWrite[actor]=true;h.hooks.failProofRead[actor]=new Set([1]);
+      const {value,pauses}=await withClock(async()=>{const r=await h.write("acknowledgeSeason",rival,nextOp(),1);assert.equal(r.ok,true);},()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor));
+      assert.deepEqual(pauses,[400]);assert.equal(value.acknowledged,true);assert.equal(value.result.phase,"ACKNOWLEDGED");
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:2,proofReads:2,adapterReads:2,committed:1},"A3b");
+      ok(`A3b ${tag} first proving read unavailable and rival lands during the pause: still converges with the same ceilings`);
+    }
+
+    // A4 no advance at all: both proving reads unadvanced; the genuine denial surfaces, no false success, no second write.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();h.hooks.denyNextWrite[actor]=true;
+      const direct=await withClock(null,()=>h.write("acknowledgeSeason",actor,nextOp(),1));
+      assert.deepEqual(direct.pauses,[400]);assert.equal(direct.value.ok,false);assert.equal(direct.value.code,"permission-denied","an unproven denial is never remapped to STALE");
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:2,adapterReads:0,committed:0},"A4 provider");
+      h.reset();h.hooks.denyNextWrite[actor]=true;
+      const viaAdapter=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor).then(()=>({threw:false}),error=>({threw:true,code:error.code})));
+      assert.deepEqual(viaAdapter.value,{threw:true,code:"permission-denied"},"the adapter shows the error instead of a success");
+      assert.deepEqual(viaAdapter.pauses,[400]);
+      assert.equal(h.store.get(h.commitPath).revision,1);assert.deepEqual(h.store.get(h.commitPath).acknowledgedRoles,[]);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:2,adapterReads:1,committed:0},"A4 adapter");
+      ok(`A4 ${tag} no advance on either proving read: permission-denied surfaces, no false success, one write, two proving reads`);
+    }
+
+    // A4b both proving reads fail (unavailable) with no advance: the original denial surfaces, still bounded.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();h.hooks.denyNextWrite[actor]=true;h.hooks.failProofRead[actor]=new Set([1,2]);
+      const {value,pauses}=await withClock(null,()=>h.write("acknowledgeSeason",actor,nextOp(),1));
+      assert.deepEqual(pauses,[400]);assert.equal(value.code,"permission-denied");
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:2,adapterReads:0,committed:0},"A4b");
+      ok(`A4b ${tag} both proving reads unavailable: the original permission-denied surfaces after exactly two reads`);
+    }
+
+    // A5 genuine revocation: the actor's device is revoked between its read and its commit. The Rules deny, both proving
+    // reads fail authority (device inactive), the denial surfaces and nothing loops.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();
+      h.hooks.afterCommitRead={role:actor,run:async()=>{h.revoke(actor);}};
+      const {value,pauses}=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor).then(()=>({threw:false}),error=>({threw:true,code:error.code})));
+      assert.deepEqual(value,{threw:true,code:"permission-denied"},"a revoked device is never told to retry or shown success");
+      assert.deepEqual(pauses,[400]);assert.equal(h.store.get(h.commitPath).revision,1);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:2,adapterReads:1,committed:0},"A5");
+      ok(`A5 ${tag} revoked device: permission-denied surfaces after one write and two proving reads, no retry loop`);
+    }
+
+    // A5b revocation plus a real rival advance during the pause: the revoked actor cannot prove anything, denial stays.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();
+      h.hooks.afterCommitRead={role:actor,run:async()=>{h.revoke(actor);}};
+      const {value,pauses}=await withClock(async()=>{const r=await h.write("acknowledgeSeason",rival,nextOp(),1);assert.equal(r.ok,true);},()=>h.write("acknowledgeSeason",actor,nextOp(),1));
+      assert.deepEqual(pauses,[400]);assert.equal(value.ok,false);assert.equal(value.code,"permission-denied");
+      assert.equal(h.store.get(h.commitPath).revision,2);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:2,adapterReads:0,committed:0},"A5b");
+      ok(`A5b ${tag} revoked device with a rival advance during the pause: still permission-denied, never STALE`);
+    }
+
+    // A6 lost write response surfaced as a transport error: the server applied the acknowledgement. No proving read runs
+    // (not a denial), and the next tap converges idempotently: the adapter's first read sees it acknowledged, and a replay
+    // of the same operation is a no-op.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();const operationId=nextOp();h.hooks.lostResponse[actor]="unavailable";
+      const first=await withClock(null,()=>h.write("acknowledgeSeason",actor,operationId,1));
+      assert.equal(first.value.ok,false);assert.equal(first.value.code,"unavailable","a transport error is not reported as success or STALE");
+      assert.deepEqual(first.pauses,[]);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:0,adapterReads:0,committed:1},"A6 lost");
+      assert.equal(h.store.get(h.commitPath).revision,2);assert.deepEqual(h.store.get(h.commitPath).acknowledgedRoles,[actor]);
+      h.reset();
+      const replay=await withClock(null,()=>h.write("acknowledgeSeason",actor,operationId,1));
+      assert.equal(replay.value.ok,true,JSON.stringify(replay.value));assert.equal(replay.value.replayed,true);assert.equal(replay.value.revision,2);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:0,adapterReads:0,committed:0},"A6 replay");
+      h.reset();
+      const tap=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor));
+      assert.deepEqual(tap.value,{acknowledged:true,attempts:0});
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:0,proofReads:0,adapterReads:1,committed:0},"A6 next tap");
+      assert.deepEqual(h.store.get(h.commitPath).acknowledgedRoles,[actor]);
+      ok(`A6 ${tag} lost response (unavailable) after a server-side write: no false STALE, same-operation replay and next tap are no-ops`);
+    }
+
+    // A6b lost response surfaced as permission-denied: the first proving read already sees the actor's own write
+    // (revision 2), so the adapter's STALE re-read finds it acknowledged and stops without a second write or a pause.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();h.hooks.lostResponse[actor]="permission-denied";
+      const {value,pauses}=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor));
+      assert.deepEqual(pauses,[]);assert.equal(value.acknowledged,true);assert.equal(value.viaRead,true);
+      assert.equal(h.store.get(h.commitPath).revision,2);assert.deepEqual(h.store.get(h.commitPath).acknowledgedRoles,[actor]);
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:1,proofReads:1,adapterReads:2,committed:1},"A6b");
+      ok(`A6b ${tag} lost response surfaced as permission-denied converges after one write and one re-read, no duplicate write`);
+    }
+
+    // A6c lost response, then the rival lands; the actor's next tap (fresh operation) sees its own acknowledgement.
+    {
+      const h=await committed();h.hooks.lostResponse[actor]="deadline-exceeded";
+      const lost=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor).then(()=>({threw:false}),error=>({threw:true,code:error.code})));
+      assert.deepEqual(lost.value,{threw:true,code:"deadline-exceeded"});assert.deepEqual(lost.pauses,[]);
+      const rivalTap=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,rival));assert.equal(rivalTap.value.acknowledged,true);assert.equal(rivalTap.value.result.phase,"ACKNOWLEDGED");
+      h.reset();
+      const again=await withClock(null,()=>adapterAcknowledge(h,actor));
+      assert.deepEqual(again.value,{acknowledged:true,attempts:0});
+      ceilings(h,actor,{writes:0,proofReads:0,adapterReads:1,committed:0},"A6c");
+      assert.deepEqual(h.store.get(h.commitPath).acknowledgedRoles,[actor,rival]);
+      ok(`A6c ${tag} lost response then rival acknowledgement: the actor's next tap sees ACKNOWLEDGED with no extra write`);
+    }
+  }
+
+  console.log(`PASS R85 acknowledgement second proving read (${checks} checks): an unproven first read waits 400 ms (controlled clock) and reads exactly once more; a late rival advance converges in one bounded retry, unproven or unauthorized denials surface, lost responses converge idempotently, in both manager orders.`);
+})().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;});
