+'use strict';
+// r85 audit F04: the Settings "About N reads today / estimate" counter must see every Firestore read route, not only the
+// budget layer's transactions. Based on the independent auditor's meter probe (Showdown_Independent_Deep_Audit_R84.md,
+// "The quota counter misses career-history reads": 100 finished Showdowns, 601 getDoc calls, readsToday = 0).
+// Real budget layer, career index, completed reader, loader, adapter and career model; only SDK IO is fake.
+// What is counted (js/sparkPrivatePairing.js, "ONE read meter"): requested server document reads. One per returned
+// document (a missing document is still one), a query answer at least one, a cache-sourced snapshot (metadata.fromCache)
+// or a listener's pending-write echo 0, a request the server answered with an error (denied...) 1, an unanswered one 0.
+const assert=require('node:assert/strict');
+const fs=require('node:fs');
+const path=require('node:path');
+const vm=require('node:vm');
+const {webcrypto,createHash}=require('node:crypto');
+const ROOT=path.resolve(__dirname,'../..');
+const read=file=>fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT,file),'utf8');
+const raw=read('tests/fixtures/saved-showdowns/r79-1season-win.json');
+const original=JSON.parse(raw).rivalryId;
+const rid=n=>'pair_'+n.toString(16).padStart(64,'0');
+const budgetSource=(source=>source.slice(source.indexOf('// Firestore budget layer (r75).')))(read('js/sparkPrivatePairing.js'));
+let checks=0;const check=(label,fn)=>{fn();checks++;};
+
+// ---- fixture (same construction as r83-cold-career-reads-contracts) ----
+function decode(value){
+  const kind=Object.keys(value)[0];
+  switch(kind){
+    case 'nullValue':return null;
+    case 'booleanValue':return value.booleanValue;
+    case 'stringValue':return value.stringValue;
+    case 'integerValue':return Number(value.integerValue);
+    case 'doubleValue':return value.doubleValue;
+    case 'timestampValue':return {toMillis:()=>Date.parse(value.timestampValue)};
+    case 'arrayValue':return (value.arrayValue.values||[]).map(decode);
+    case 'mapValue':return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value.mapValue.fields||{}).map(([k,v])=>[k,decode(v)]));
+    default:throw new Error(kind);
+  }
+}
+function canonical(value){
+  if(value&&typeof value.toMillis==='function')return {$timestamp:value.toMillis()};
+  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.map(canonical);
+  if(value&&typeof value==='object')return Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(value).sort().map(k=>[k,canonical(value[k])]));
+  return value;
+}
+function game(n,total=1){
+  const f=JSON.parse(raw.replaceAll(original,rid(n)));
+  const docs=Object.fromEntries(f.documents.map(d=>[d.path,decode({mapValue:{fields:d.fields}})]));
+  const root=docs['rivalries/'+rid(n)];
+  if(total!==1){
+    docs[`rivalries/${rid(n)}/sharedSetup/authoritative`].totalSeasons=total;
+    Object.assign(root.data.terminalClose,{totalSeasons:total,completedSeason:total,managerTotals:{playerOne:5*total,playerTwo:total}});
+    Object.assign(root.data.terminalProgress,{totalSeasons:total,acceptedThroughSeason:total,managerTotals:{playerOne:5*total,playerTwo:total}});
+    for(let season=2;season<=total;season++)for(const suffix of ['seasonResults/season_1','seasonResults/season_1/roles/playerOne','seasonResults/season_1/roles/playerTwo','seasonCommits/season_1']){
+      const value=docs[`rivalries/${rid(n)}/${suffix}`];
+      docs[`rivalries/${rid(n)}/${suffix.replace('season_1',`season_${season}`)}`]={...value,seasonNumber:season};
+    }
+  }
+  root.contentHash='sha256:'+createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(canonical({objectType:'rivalry',objectId:rid(n),revision:root.revision,data:root.data}))).digest('hex');
+  return docs;
+}
+const accounts=game(1)['rivalries/'+rid(1)].data.managerSlots.map(s=>s.accountId);
+const envelope=(type,id,data)=>({schemaVersion:1,objectType:type,objectId:id,revision:0,lifecycleState:'live',contentHash:'sha256:fixture',data});
+function index(docs,uid,ids){docs[`accounts/${uid}/careerIndex/current`]=envelope('careerIndex','current',{rivalryIds:ids,sealedPageCount:0});}
+function complete(result,count,total){
+  assert.equal(result.status,'ready');
+  assert.equal(result.model.coverage.readable,count);
+  assert.equal(result.model.history.showdowns.length,count);
+  assert.equal(result.model.managers.daniel.careerPoints,5*count*total);
+  assert.equal(result.model.managers.nik.careerPoints,count*total);
+}
+// One fresh page: the real modules plus the real budget layer in one realm; `log` holds every server get the SDK received.
+function page(docs){
+  const db={},log=[];let active=0,peak=0;
+  const rawSdk={doc:(_db,...p)=>p.join('/'),getDoc:async ref=>{
+    log.push(ref);active++;peak=Math.max(peak,active);
+    try{await new Promise(resolve=>setImmediate(resolve));const value=docs[ref];return {exists:()=>value!==undefined,data:()=>value,metadata:{fromCache:false,hasPendingWrites:false}};}
+    finally{active--;}
+  }};
+  const c=vm.createContext({console,TextEncoder,crypto:webcrypto,setTimeout,clearTimeout});
+  vm.runInContext(read('js/persistentNikDanielPair.js'),c);
+  for(const [key,file] of Object.entries({CareerModeSparkCompletedShowdownReader:'sparkCompletedShowdownReader',CareerModeSharedClosedShowdownAdapter:'sharedClosedShowdownAdapter',CareerModeSharedCareerAnalytics:'sharedCareerAnalytics'}))c[key]=require(path.join(ROOT,'js',file+'.js'));
+  vm.runInContext(read('js/sparkClosedShowdownCareerLoader.js'),c);
+  vm.runInContext(budgetSource,c);
+  const budget=c.CareerModeFirestoreBudget;
+  return {c,db,log,rawSdk,budget,peak:()=>peak,reads:()=>budget.readsToday().reads};
+}
+const noTransaction=async()=>{throw new Error('archive reads use getDoc, never a transaction');};
+
+// ---- 1. archives: 1/10/100 finished Showdowns, one and ten seasons, both managers, cold then warm ----
+const measured={};
+async function archives(){
+  for(const total of [1,10])for(const count of [1,10,100])for(const uid of (count===100&&total===10?[accounts[0]]:accounts)){
+    const docs=Object.assign({},...Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>game(i+1,total)));
+    index(docs,uid,Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>rid(i+1)));
+    const p=page(docs);
+    // The runtime path: services(...) hands out the metered SDK (the auditor's probe used exactly this route).
+    const services=p.budget.services({ok:true,auth:{currentUser:{uid}},firestore:p.db,firestoreSdk:{...p.rawSdk,runTransaction:noTransaction}});
+    const options={firestore:p.db,firebaseSdk:services.firestoreSdk,user:{uid},cryptoImpl:webcrypto};
+    const load=()=>p.c.CareerModeSparkClosedShowdownCareerLoader.loadClosedShowdownCareer(options);
+    complete(await load(),count,total);
+    const cold=p.log.length;
+    assert.equal(cold,1+(2+4*total)*count,`cold ${count}x${total}: one index head plus 2 + 4 per season for every Showdown`);
+    assert.equal(p.reads(),cold,`cold ${count}x${total}: readsToday equals the server gets (was 0 before r85)`);
+    assert.ok(p.peak()>1&&p.peak()<=12,'metering leaves the loader concurrency unchanged');
+    complete(await load(),count,total);
+    const warm=p.log.length-cold;
+    assert.equal(warm,1,'warm visit asks the server for the mutable index head only');
+    assert.equal(p.reads()-cold,warm,'warm visit: finished Showdowns served from memory count 0 new reads');
+    assert.equal(p.budget.stats().directReads,p.log.length);
+    if(uid===accounts[0])measured[`${count}x${total}`]={cold,coldReads:cold,warm,warmReads:warm};
+  }
+  checks++;
+}
+
+// ---- 2. the module-import path (rivalryLegacyV10, seasonFinalV10, careerStartMarker) counts once, never twice ----
+async function importPath(){
+  const uid=accounts[1],docs=Object.assign({},game(1),game(2));index(docs,uid,[rid(1),rid(2)]);
+  const p=page(docs),M=p.budget.meterSdk;
+  // runtime services expose no getDoc: the screens import it from the Firestore module and pass it through meterSdk.
+  const services=p.budget.services({ok:true,auth:{currentUser:{uid}},firestore:p.db,firestoreSdk:{doc:p.rawSdk.doc,runTransaction:noTransaction}});
+  assert.equal(typeof services.firestoreSdk.getDoc,'undefined');
+  const sdk=M(services.firestoreSdk?.getDoc?services.firestoreSdk:p.rawSdk);
+  assert.equal(M(sdk),sdk,'meterSdk is idempotent');
+  assert.equal(M(sdk).getDoc,p.budget.meterGet(sdk.getDoc),'a metered function is never wrapped again');
+  const twice=p.budget.services({ok:true,auth:{currentUser:{uid}},firestore:p.db,firestoreSdk:{...sdk,runTransaction:noTransaction}});
+  assert.equal(twice.firestoreSdk.getDoc,sdk.getDoc,'services(...) keeps an already metered getDoc');
+  const result=await p.c.CareerModeSparkClosedShowdownCareerLoader.loadClosedShowdownCareer({firestore:p.db,firebaseSdk:{...twice.firestoreSdk,getDoc:sdk.getDoc},user:{uid},cryptoImpl:webcrypto});
+  complete(result,2,1);
+  assert.equal(p.reads(),p.log.length,'one count per server get through the import path');
+  assert.equal(p.log.length,13);
+  // Career index read on its own (career marker / Home path) is metered the same way.
+  const before=p.reads(),gets=p.log.length;
+  await p.c.CareerModePersistentNikDanielPair.readCareerIndex({firestore:p.db,firebaseSdk:sdk,accountId:uid});
+  assert.equal(p.reads()-before,p.log.length-gets);
+  checks++;
+}
+
+// ---- 3. what one request counts ----
+async function units(){
+  const p=page({}),b=p.budget,snap=(fromCache,pending=false)=>({exists:()=>true,data:()=>({}),metadata:{fromCache,hasPendingWrites:pending}});
+  check('server document 1, cache 0',()=>{assert.equal(b.readUnits(snap(false)),1);assert.equal(b.readUnits(snap(true)),0);assert.equal(b.readUnits({exists:()=>false,data:()=>undefined,metadata:{fromCache:false}}),1);});
+  check('query: 1 per document, at least 1',()=>{assert.equal(b.readUnits({docs:[],metadata:{fromCache:false}}),1);assert.equal(b.readUnits({docs:[1,2,3],metadata:{fromCache:false}}),3);assert.equal(b.readUnits({docs:[1,2],metadata:{fromCache:true}}),0);});
+  check('listener pending-write echo 0',()=>{assert.equal(b.readUnits(snap(false,true),true),0);assert.equal(b.readUnits(snap(false,true)),1);});
+  const error=code=>Object.assign(new Error(code),{code});
+  const answers=[snap(true),snap(false),{docs:[1,2,3,4],metadata:{fromCache:false}},error('permission-denied'),error('unavailable'),error('resource-exhausted'),error('cancelled'),new Error('client side')];
+  const sdk=b.meterSdk({doc:()=>'x',getDoc:async()=>{const a=answers.shift();if(a instanceof Error)throw a;return a;},getDocs:async()=>{const a=answers.shift();if(a instanceof Error)throw a;return a;}});
+  const start=b.readsToday().reads;
+  await sdk.getDoc('x');assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,0,'cache-sourced get counts 0');
+  await sdk.getDoc('x');assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,1,'server get counts 1');
+  await sdk.getDocs('q');assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,5,'query of 4 documents counts 4');
+  await assert.rejects(sdk.getDoc('x'));assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,6,'denied get counts 1, as Firestore bills it');
+  for(let i=0;i<4;i++)await assert.rejects(sdk.getDoc('x'));
+  assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,6,'unanswered (offline, quota, cancelled) or client-side failures count 0');
+  checks++;
+}
+
+// ---- 4. listeners: unowned listeners metered through meterSdk; both callback shapes ----
+async function listeners(){
+  const p=page({}),b=p.budget;let next=null,fail=null;
+  const sdk=b.meterSdk({doc:()=>'x',onSnapshot:(ref,options,a,e)=>{if(typeof a==='object'){next=v=>a.next(v);fail=v=>a.error(v);}else{next=a;fail=e;}return ()=>{};}});
+  const start=b.readsToday().reads;let seen=0;
+  sdk.onSnapshot('x',{includeMetadataChanges:true},()=>{seen++;},()=>{seen++;});
+  next({exists:()=>true,data:()=>({}),metadata:{fromCache:true,hasPendingWrites:false}});
+  next({exists:()=>true,data:()=>({}),metadata:{fromCache:false,hasPendingWrites:false}});
+  next({exists:()=>true,data:()=>({}),metadata:{fromCache:false,hasPendingWrites:true}});
+  fail(Object.assign(new Error('denied'),{code:'permission-denied'}));
+  assert.equal(seen,4,'every callback still runs');
+  assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,2,'cache 0 + server 1 + own pending write 0 + denied listen 1');
+  sdk.onSnapshot('x',{includeMetadataChanges:true},{next:()=>{seen++;},error:()=>{seen++;}});
+  next({exists:()=>true,data:()=>({}),metadata:{fromCache:false,hasPendingWrites:false}});
+  fail(Object.assign(new Error('offline'),{code:'unavailable'}));
+  assert.equal(seen,6);assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,3,'observer form metered the same way');
+  // countListenerRead stays (public API) and still counts one.
+  b.countListenerRead();assert.equal(b.readsToday().reads-start,4);
+  checks++;
+}
+
+// ---- 5. source census: every getDoc/getDocs/onSnapshot call site in js/ is covered or explicitly excluded ----
+function census(){
+  const covered={
+    'sparkPrivatePairing.js':'budget layer: services() meters getDoc/getDocs/onSnapshot; owned listeners count in fsbCtxDelivery/fsbWaitDelivery',
+    'rivalryLegacyV10.js':'career marker + index + loader: getDoc via meterSdk',
+    'seasonFinalV10.js':'completed history reader: getDoc via meterSdk',
+    'careerStartMarker.js':'resolveServices: getDoc via meterSdk; readMarker/careerHiddenRivalryIds use the sdk they are given',
+    'productionSharedSeasonCommit.js':'season-commit wait context listeners: onSnapshot via meterSdk',
+    'persistentNikDanielPair.js':'Home JOIN note listener via meterSdk; readCareerIndex uses the sdk it is given (metered by callers)',
+    'sparkClosedShowdownCareerLoader.js':'uses options.firebaseSdk.getDoc (metered by callers); its cancel wrapper calls the metered function',
+    'sparkCompletedShowdownReader.js':'uses options.firebaseSdk.getDoc (metered by callers)',
+    'ssjrPhysicalJourneyAcceptance.js':'SSJR test panel closed-Showdown check (index + one exact read): getDoc via meterSdk',
+    'sparkSharedSeasonResults.js':'uses sdk.getDoc only when the given (budget-wrapped, metered) services expose it; else a counted transaction'
+  };
+  const excluded={
+    'sparkSharedShowdownSetup.js':'mentions getDoc in a comment only; its preflight read is a counted transaction',
+    'sparkCompletedTransferHistoryReader.js':'no caller in js/; any caller passing budget services gets a metered getDoc',
+    'productionAuthorizationAcceptance.js':'operator acceptance page (network-only, never loaded by the app page or its counter)',
+    'productionProviderAbuseAcceptance.js':'operator acceptance page (network-only, never loaded by the app page or its counter)'
+  };
+  const pattern=/\b(?:getDocs?|getDocFromServer|getDocsFromServer|onSnapshot)\b/;
+  const found=fs.readdirSync(path.join(ROOT,'js')).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.js')&&pattern.test(read('js/'+f).replace(/captureCareerModeRawSaveLibraryMigrationSnapshot|captureLibraryMigrationSnapshot/g,'')));
+  assert.deepEqual(found.sort(),Object.keys({...covered,...excluded}).sort(),'a new Firestore read call site must be metered and listed here');
+  for(const file of ['rivalryLegacyV10.js','seasonFinalV10.js','careerStartMarker.js','productionSharedSeasonCommit.js','persistentNikDanielPair.js','ssjrPhysicalJourneyAcceptance.js']){
+    const source=read('js/'+file);
+    assert.ok(source.includes('CareerModeFirestoreBudget?.meterSdk')||source.includes('budget?.meterSdk'),`${file} meters the SDK it takes`);
+    assert.ok(!/countListenerRead/.test(source),`${file} no longer counts listener deliveries by hand (would count twice)`);
+  }
+  for(const line of read('js/rivalryLegacyV10.js').split('\n').concat(read('js/seasonFinalV10.js').split('\n'),read('js/careerStartMarker.js').split('\n')))
+    if(/import\(runtime\.firebaseFirestoreModule\)/.test(line))assert.match(line,/meterSdk/,'every getDoc taken from the Firestore module is metered');
+  const settings=read('js/settings.js');
+  assert.match(settings,/`About \$\{rounded/);assert.match(settings,/estimate until a season is played today/);
+  assert.match(budgetSource,/FSB_BILLED_PER_READ=2\.8/,'the 2.8 billed-per-read estimate factor is unchanged');
+  checks++;
+}
+
+(async()=>{
+  await archives();await importPath();await units();await listeners();census();
+  const m=measured['100x1'],t=measured['100x10'];
+  console.log(`PASS r85 reads counter contracts: ${checks} checks; 100 one-season cold ${m.cold} gets -> readsToday ${m.coldReads} (was 0), warm ${m.warm}/${m.warmReads}; 100 ten-season cold ${t.cold} -> ${t.coldReads}, warm ${t.warm}/${t.warmReads}`);
+})().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;});

+"use strict";
+// Audit R84 F01/F02/F03 (r85): static contract for the shared write authority and its raw-SDK denial matrix.
+// F01 (writes only, decided): every shared write requires the ACTING account to be active. Reads stay membership-only.
+// F02: Season Commit create and acknowledge require the Session/device/account authority (they checked ID format only).
+// F03: a raw-SDK denial matrix on the composed generated Rules runs in the Gate and in Validate Gameplay Fast.
+const assert=require("node:assert/strict");
+const fs=require("node:fs");
+const path=require("node:path");
+
+const root=path.resolve(__dirname,"../..");
+const read=file=>fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,file),"utf8");
+let n=0;
+const ok=text=>{n+=1;process.stdout.write(`ok ${n} ${text}\n`);};
+function functionBody(source,name){
+  const start=source.indexOf(`    function ${name}(`);
+  assert.ok(start>=0,`function ${name} must exist`);
+  const end=source.indexOf("\n    }\n",start);
+  assert.ok(end>start,`function ${name} must close`);
+  return source.slice(start,end);
+}
+
+const setup=read("firestore.shared-setup-production.fragment.rules");
+const career=read("firestore.career-start-production.fragment.rules");
+const transfer=read("firestore.transfer-challenge-production.fragment.rules");
+const results=read("firestore.season-results-production.fragment.rules");
+const commit=read("firestore.season-commit-production.fragment.rules");
+
+// A1: the actor authority looks up the acting account and the acting device and checks the Session binding.
+{
+  const actor=functionBody(setup,"ssjrActorWriteAuthorityValid");
+  for(const needle of [
+    "let actorAccount = get(/databases/$(database)/documents/accounts/$(actor));",
+    "let device = get(/databases/$(database)/documents/accounts/$(actor)/devices/$(deviceId));",
+    "let session = get(/databases/$(database)/documents/rivalries/$(rivalryId)/sessions/$(sessionId));",
+    "&& actor in rivalry.authorizedAccountIds",
+    "&& actorAccount.data.data.status == 'active'",
+    "&& device.data.objectId == deviceId",
+    "&& device.data.data.state == 'active'",
+    "&& sessionData.rivalryId == rivalryId",
+    "&& sessionData.state == 'active'",
+    "&& rivalry.authorizedAccountIds[0] in sessionData.memberAccountIds",
+    "&& rivalry.authorizedAccountIds[1] in sessionData.memberAccountIds",
+    "&& sessionData.expiresAt is timestamp;"
+  ])assert.ok(actor.includes(needle),`ssjrActorWriteAuthorityValid must keep: ${needle}`);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(actor,/sessionData\.expiresAt\s*>/,"an ACTIVE Session past its old expiresAt must stay usable (ten-season continuity)");
+  assert.equal((actor.match(/get\(/g)||[]).length,4,"the actor authority looks up exactly four documents: rivalry, acting account, acting device, Session");
+  const write=functionBody(setup,"ssjrWriteAuthorityValid");
+  assert.match(write,/return ssjrActorWriteAuthorityValid\(rivalryId, deviceId, sessionId\)\n/,"ssjrWriteAuthorityValid must include the actor authority");
+  ok("A1 the shared actor write authority looks up the acting account, the acting device and the Session (four documents) and keeps ACTIVE-past-expiry continuity");
+}
+// A2: every shared write validator reaches the authority.
+{
+  const writers=[
+    [setup,"ssjrValidLedgerBase","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, root.updatedByDeviceId, root.activeSessionId)"],
+    [career,"ssjrCareerWriteValid","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, root.updatedByDeviceId, root.activeSessionId)"],
+    [career,"ssjrCareerValidUpdate","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, after.updatedByDeviceId, after.activeSessionId)"],
+    [transfer,"ssjrTransferPublicShape","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, root.updatedByDeviceId, root.activeSessionId)"],
+    [transfer,"ssjrTransferValidUpdate","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, after.updatedByDeviceId, after.activeSessionId)"],
+    [transfer,"ssjrTransferPrivateBase","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, root.updatedByDeviceId, root.activeSessionId)"],
+    [transfer,"ssjrTransferPrivateUpdateValid","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, after.updatedByDeviceId, after.activeSessionId)"],
+    [results,"ssjrResultsPublicShape","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, root.updatedByDeviceId, root.activeSessionId)"],
+    [results,"ssjrResultsValidUpdate","ssjrWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, after.updatedByDeviceId, after.activeSessionId)"],
+    [commit,"ssjrCommitValidCreate","ssjrActorWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, after.updatedByDeviceId, after.activeSessionId)"],
+    [commit,"ssjrCommitValidUpdate","ssjrActorWriteAuthorityValid(rivalryId, after.updatedByDeviceId, after.activeSessionId)"]
+  ];
+  for(const [source,name,call] of writers)assert.ok(functionBody(source,name).includes(call),`${name} must call ${call}`);
+  assert.match(functionBody(setup,"ssjrLeagueProjectionCreateValid"),/return activeActor\(\)\n/,"the league projection create must require the acting account to be active");
+  // The role-private Season Results create is valid only together with its public document in the same batch
+  // (getAfter + publishedAt == request.time), and that public write carries the authority above.
+  const privateCreate=functionBody(results,"ssjrResultsPrivateCreateValid");
+  assert.match(privateCreate,/getAfter\(\/databases\/\$\(database\)\/documents\/rivalries\/\$\(rivalryId\)\/seasonResults\/\$\(seasonId\)\)/);
+  assert.ok(privateCreate.includes("&& public.updatedAt == request.time"),"role-private result must be written with its public document");
+  assert.equal(/function ssjrCommitShape\(/.test(commit),false,"no never-called Season Commit helper may remain");
+  ok(`A2 all ${writers.length} shared write validators reach the actor authority; the league projection checks the acting account; no dead Season Commit helper`);
+}
+// A3: reads stay membership-only by decision (audit R84 F01 triage: a lookup per listener read would cost hundreds of reads a season).
+{
+  const entitled=functionBody(setup,"ssjrEntitled");
+  assert.equal(/accounts\//.test(entitled),false,"ssjrEntitled must not look up an account");
+  for(const [source,label] of [[setup,"Shared Setup"],[career,"Career Start"],[transfer,"Transfer Challenge"],[results,"Season Results"],[commit,"Season Commit"]]){
+    assert.ok(source.includes("allow get: if ssjrEntitled(rivalryId);"),`${label} reads stay on ssjrEntitled`);
+  }
+  ok("A3 reads stay membership-only (ssjrEntitled, one rivalry lookup) by decision");
+}
+// A4: the raw-SDK denial matrix exists, asserts unchanged bytes, and runs in both CI lanes.
+{
+  const file="tests/firebase/season-commit-raw-authority-denial-emulator.cjs";
+  const matrix=read(file);
+  for(const id of ["P1","P2","D1","D2","D3","D4","D5","D6","D7","D8","D9","D10","D11","R1","C1","C2","C3","C4"])assert.ok(matrix.includes(`"${id}"`),`matrix case ${id}`);
+  for(const kind of ["revoked-device","unregistered-device","deletion-pending-actor","nonexistent-session","revoked-session","foreign-rivalry-session","wrong-member-session","stranger"])assert.ok(matrix.includes(`kind:"${kind}"`),`matrix kind ${kind}`);
+  assert.match(matrix,/permission-denied/);
+  assert.match(matrix,/canonical\(after\),canonical\(before\)/,"each denial must compare stored bytes before and after");
+  assert.match(matrix,/shared-season-commit-concurrent-acknowledge-emulator\.cjs/,"the matrix reuses the concurrent-acknowledge fixture");
+  assert.match(read("tests/firebase/shared-season-commit-concurrent-acknowledge-emulator.cjs"),/readFileSync\("firestore\.spark\.generated\.rules","utf8"\)/,"the fixture loads the composed generated Rules");
+  for(const workflow of [".github/workflows/showdown-gate.yml",".github/workflows/validate-gameplay-fast.yml"]){
+    const line=read(workflow).split("\n").find(text=>text.includes(file)&&text.includes("emulators:exec"));
+    assert.ok(line,`${workflow} must run ${file}`);
+  }
+  assert.ok(read("tests/firebase/composed-production-rules-regression.cjs").includes(`"${file}"`),"the composed regression labels the matrix");
+  ok("A4 the raw-SDK denial matrix (18 cases, unchanged-bytes assertions) runs in the Gate L3A lane, Validate Gameplay Fast and the composed regression");
+}
+process.stdout.write(`PASS shared write authority contracts: ${n} numbered checks (audit R84 F01 writes, F02 Season Commit authority, F03 raw denial matrix wiring)\n`);

+"use strict";
+
+// Audit R84 F03 (r85): raw-SDK denial matrix for the Season Commit write boundary on the composed generated Rules.
+// Run inside ONE emulators:exec (firestore only):
+//   npx --yes firebase-tools@15.28.1 emulators:exec --only firestore --project demo-cms-season-commit-raw-authority \
+//     "node tests/firebase/season-commit-raw-authority-denial-emulator.cjs"
+//
+// The provider suites (for example concurrent-acknowledge R3) prove that the app refuses a revoked device before it
+// writes. That does not prove the server boundary: a member can bypass the provider and call setDoc directly. Here the
+// real providers drive a valid revision-1 committed season, a wrapper captures the protocol-generated next
+// acknowledgement (or the coordinator's create) WITHOUT committing it, one authority fact is changed, and the captured
+// payload is sent straight to Firestore with the client SDK. Every invalid case must be permission-denied AND leave the
+// stored commit byte-identical (same revision, same acknowledgements). Positive controls prove the captured payload is
+// otherwise valid, so a denial is caused by the changed authority fact and nothing else.
+//
+// Fixture helpers are reused from the concurrent-acknowledge suite exactly as the R84 audit probe did: its source up to
+// the first async runner is compiled together with this file's matrix, so the two suites share one fixture.
+
+const fs=require("node:fs");
+const path=require("node:path");
+const Module=require("node:module");
+
+if(!process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST)throw new Error("FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST is required: run this suite inside firebase emulators:exec.");
+if(!String(process.env.GCLOUD_PROJECT||"demo-cms-season-commit-raw-authority").startsWith("demo-"))throw new Error("A demo- project is required; production is forbidden.");
+if(!process.env.GCLOUD_PROJECT)process.env.GCLOUD_PROJECT="demo-cms-season-commit-raw-authority";
+
+const fixtureFile=path.join(__dirname,"shared-season-commit-concurrent-acknowledge-emulator.cjs");
+const fixtureSource=fs.readFileSync(fixtureFile,"utf8");
+const runnerAt=fixtureSource.indexOf("\n(async()=>{");
+if(runnerAt<0)throw new Error("concurrent-acknowledge fixture runner marker not found");
+const fixturePrefix=fixtureSource.slice(0,runnerAt);
+for(const name of ["prepareCommitted","storedCommit","function account(","function device(","function session("]){
+  if(!fixturePrefix.includes(name))throw new Error(`concurrent-acknowledge fixture no longer defines ${name}`);
+}
+
+const matrix=String.raw`
+const STRANGER="acct_raw_authority_stranger";
+const R_FOREIGN="pair_"+"8".repeat(64);
+const S_FOREIGN="session_"+"6".repeat(64);
+const UNREGISTERED_DEVICE="device_"+"f".repeat(32);
+const MISSING_SESSION="session_"+"f".repeat(64);
+const COMMIT_ID="season_1";
+
+function canonical(value){
+  if(value===null||typeof value!=="object")return JSON.stringify(value);
+  if(value instanceof Timestamp)return JSON.stringify({ts:[value.seconds,value.nanoseconds]});
+  if(Array.isArray(value))return "["+value.map(canonical).join(",")+"]";
+  return "{"+Object.keys(value).sort().map(k=>JSON.stringify(k)+":"+canonical(value[k])).join(",")+"}";
+}
+function stopCapture(){return Object.assign(new Error("raw authority matrix capture only"),{code:"RAW_MATRIX_CAPTURE"});}
+// Run the real provider but take its transaction.set payload instead of committing it.
+function captureSdk(sink){
+  return {...sdk(),runTransaction:(db,fn)=>firestoreSdk.runTransaction(db,async tx=>fn({
+    get:ref=>tx.get(ref),
+    set:(ref,data)=>{sink.ref=ref;sink.data=data;throw stopCapture();},
+    update:()=>{throw stopCapture();}
+  }))};
+}
+async function captureAcknowledgement(h){
+  const sink={};
+  await Commit.acknowledgeSeason({...h.b(1100),firebaseSdk:captureSdk(sink),operationId:op("season_commit_op_",88),baseRevision:1});
+  assert.ok(sink.data,"capture a protocol-generated legitimate next acknowledgement without committing it");
+  assert.equal(sink.data.revision,2);assert.deepEqual(sink.data.acknowledgedRoles,["playerTwo"]);
+  return sink.data;
+}
+async function rulesOff(env,fn){await env.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async context=>fn(context.firestore()));}
+async function patchSession(env,ref,patch){
+  await rulesOff(env,async db=>{
+    const target=doc(db,...ref),old=(await getDoc(target)).data();
+    await setDoc(target,{...old,data:{...old.data,...patch}});
+  });
+}
+async function denied(label,write){
+  let code=null;
+  try{await write();}catch(error){code=error&&error.code;}
+  assert.equal(code,"permission-denied",label+": raw write must be permission-denied by the Rules, got "+(code||"ACCEPTED"));
+}
+
+const ACK_CASES=[
+  {id:"D1",kind:"revoked-device",label:"acting device revoked",mutate:async env=>rulesOff(env,db=>setDoc(doc(db,"accounts",B,"devices",DB),device(DB,"revoked")))},
+  {id:"D2",kind:"unregistered-device",label:"device ID never registered to the acting account",payload:p=>({...p,updatedByDeviceId:UNREGISTERED_DEVICE})},
+  {id:"D3",kind:"partner-device",label:"device registered to the PARTNER account, not the actor",payload:p=>({...p,updatedByDeviceId:DA})},
+  {id:"D4",kind:"deletion-pending-actor",label:"acting account deletion-pending",mutate:async env=>rulesOff(env,db=>setDoc(doc(db,"accounts",B),{...account(B),data:{status:"deletion-pending"}}))},
+  {id:"D5",kind:"nonexistent-session",label:"Session ID that does not exist",payload:p=>({...p,activeSessionId:MISSING_SESSION})},
+  {id:"D6",kind:"revoked-session",label:"Session state revoked",mutate:async env=>patchSession(env,["rivalries",R,"sessions",S],{state:"revoked"})},
+  {id:"D7",kind:"closed-session",label:"Session state closed",mutate:async env=>patchSession(env,["rivalries",R,"sessions",S],{state:"closed"})},
+  {id:"D8",kind:"foreign-rivalry-session",label:"ACTIVE Session that belongs to another rivalry",
+    mutate:async env=>rulesOff(env,async db=>{
+      const foreign=session(Date.now());foreign.objectId=S_FOREIGN;foreign.data={...foreign.data,rivalryId:R_FOREIGN};
+      await setDoc(doc(db,"rivalries",R_FOREIGN,"sessions",S_FOREIGN),foreign);
+    }),
+    payload:p=>({...p,activeSessionId:S_FOREIGN})},
+  {id:"D9",kind:"rebound-session",label:"Session document under this rivalry but bound to another rivalry",mutate:async env=>patchSession(env,["rivalries",R,"sessions",S],{rivalryId:R_FOREIGN})},
+  {id:"D10",kind:"wrong-member-session",label:"ACTIVE Session whose members are not both managers",mutate:async env=>patchSession(env,["rivalries",R,"sessions",S],{memberAccountIds:[A,STRANGER]})},
+  {id:"D11",kind:"stranger",label:"unrelated signed-in account (control)",actor:env=>env.authenticatedContext(STRANGER).firestore()}
+];
+
+(async()=>{
+  const env=await initializeTestEnvironment({projectId:PROJECT_ID,firestore:{rules:RULES}});
+  let n=0;
+  const ok=(id,text)=>{n+=1;process.stdout.write("ok "+n+" "+id+" "+text+"\n");};
+  try{
+    // P1: the captured acknowledgement is valid as is (positive control for every D case).
+    {
+      const h=await prepareCommitted(env);
+      const payload=await captureAcknowledgement(h);
+      await setDoc(doc(h.dbB,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID),payload);
+      const stored=await storedCommit(env);
+      assert.equal(stored.revision,2);assert.deepEqual(stored.acknowledgedRoles,["playerTwo"]);
+      ok("P1","valid raw acknowledgement from the registered active device in the ACTIVE Session is accepted (revision 1 -> 2)");
+    }
+    // P2: ten-season continuity: an ACTIVE Session past its old expiresAt still accepts the acknowledgement.
+    {
+      const h=await prepareCommitted(env);
+      const payload=await captureAcknowledgement(h);
+      await patchSession(env,["rivalries",R,"sessions",S],{expiresAt:Timestamp.fromMillis(Date.now()-60_000)});
+      await setDoc(doc(h.dbB,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID),payload);
+      assert.equal((await storedCommit(env)).revision,2);
+      ok("P2","ACTIVE Session past its expiresAt still accepts the acknowledgement (supported ten-season continuity)");
+    }
+    for(const c of ACK_CASES){
+      const h=await prepareCommitted(env);
+      let payload=await captureAcknowledgement(h);
+      if(c.mutate)await c.mutate(env);
+      if(c.payload)payload=c.payload(payload);
+      const before=await storedCommit(env);
+      assert.equal(before.revision,1);
+      const actorDb=c.actor?c.actor(env):h.dbB;
+      await denied(c.id+" "+c.kind,()=>setDoc(doc(actorDb,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID),payload));
+      const after=await storedCommit(env);
+      assert.equal(after.revision,1,c.kind+": stored revision must stay 1");
+      assert.deepEqual(after.acknowledgedRoles,[],c.kind+": no acknowledgement may be recorded");
+      assert.equal(canonical(after),canonical(before),c.kind+": stored commit bytes must be unchanged");
+      ok(c.id,"raw acknowledgement with "+c.label+" is permission-denied and the stored commit is unchanged (revision 1, no ACK)");
+    }
+    // D4r: by decision (audit R84 F01 triage) reads stay membership-only; only writes check the acting account.
+    {
+      const h=await prepareCommitted(env);
+      await rulesOff(env,db=>setDoc(doc(db,"accounts",B),{...account(B),data:{status:"deletion-pending"}}));
+      const snapshot=await firestoreSdk.getDocFromServer(doc(h.dbB,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID));
+      assert.equal(snapshot.data().revision,1);
+      await assertFails(firestoreSdk.getDocFromServer(doc(env.authenticatedContext(STRANGER).firestore(),"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID)));
+      ok("R1","reads stay membership-only by decision: a deletion-pending member can still read the commit, a stranger cannot");
+    }
+    // C1/C2: the coordinator's raw create. The provider builds the create payload; the stored commit is removed first.
+    {
+      const captureCreate=async()=>{
+        const h=await prepareCommitted(env);
+        await rulesOff(env,db=>firestoreSdk.deleteDoc(doc(db,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID)));
+        const sink={};
+        await Commit.commitSeason({...h.a(1300),firebaseSdk:captureSdk(sink),operationId:op("season_commit_op_",77),baseRevision:0});
+        assert.ok(sink.data,"capture the protocol-generated create");
+        assert.equal(sink.data.revision,1);
+        return {h,payload:sink.data};
+      };
+      const exists=async()=>{let value=true;await rulesOff(env,async db=>{value=(await getDoc(doc(db,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID))).exists();});return value;};
+      for(const [id,label,change] of [
+        ["C1","a Session ID that does not exist",p=>({...p,activeSessionId:MISSING_SESSION})],
+        ["C2","an unregistered device ID",p=>({...p,updatedByDeviceId:UNREGISTERED_DEVICE})]
+      ]){
+        const {h,payload}=await captureCreate();
+        await denied(id,()=>setDoc(doc(h.dbA,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID),change(payload)));
+        assert.equal(await exists(),false,id+": no commit may be created");
+        ok(id,"raw coordinator create with "+label+" is permission-denied and no commit is stored");
+      }
+      {
+        const {h,payload}=await captureCreate();
+        await rulesOff(env,db=>setDoc(doc(db,"accounts",A),{...account(A),data:{status:"deletion-pending"}}));
+        await denied("C3",()=>setDoc(doc(h.dbA,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID),payload));
+        assert.equal(await exists(),false,"C3: no commit may be created");
+        ok("C3","raw coordinator create from a deletion-pending account is permission-denied and no commit is stored");
+      }
+      {
+        const {h,payload}=await captureCreate();
+        await setDoc(doc(h.dbA,"rivalries",R,"seasonCommits",COMMIT_ID),payload);
+        assert.equal((await storedCommit(env)).revision,1);
+        ok("C4","the same captured create, unchanged, is accepted (positive control)");
+      }
+    }
+    process.stdout.write("PASS Season Commit raw-SDK authority matrix: "+n+" numbered checks on the composed generated Rules\n");
+  }finally{await env.cleanup();}
+})().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;});
+`;
+
+// Compiled as this file (same directory as the fixture, so its relative requires resolve identically).
+const m=new Module(__filename,module);
+m.filename=__filename;
+m.paths=Module._nodeModulePaths(__dirname);
+m._compile(fixturePrefix+"\n"+matrix,__filename);

+// fragment. This diagnostic still measures those sub-expressions, so it injects the exact removed text into its own copy of

+const DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY_COMMIT_HELPERS=String.raw`    function ssjrCommitResultsReady(rivalryId, seasonId, seasonNumber) {
+      let setup = ssjrCommitSetup(rivalryId);
+      let public = ssjrCommitPublicResults(rivalryId, seasonId);

+        && root.operationHashes is list
+        && root.baseRevisions is list
+        && root.actorRoles is list
+        && root.operationIds.size() == n

+        && root.actorRoles.size() == n
+        && ssjrCommitValidOperationId(root.operationIds[0])

+        && ssjrCommitValidRole(root.actorRoles[0])

+          && root.baseRevisions[2] == 2
+          && ssjrCommitValidRole(root.actorRoles[2])));

+    function ssjrCommitAcknowledgementsValid(root) {
+      let n = root.revision;
+      return root.acknowledgedRoles is list
+        && root.acknowledgedRoles.size() == n - 1
+        && root.acknowledgedRoles.toSet().size() == root.acknowledgedRoles.size()
+        && root.acknowledgedRoles.toSet().hasOnly(['playerOne','playerTwo'])
+        && root.actorRoles == [root.actorRoles[0]].concat(root.acknowledgedRoles);
+    }
